		0 = nothing
		1 = Create Shell
		2 = Extract MeshF
		3 = Extract Band

[RoutineDef, J_Dec1,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemPreviousColor", 3, 255]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemDocRange", 1, 0]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemFloorStat", 1, 0]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemBGColor", 3, 255]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemCurrentRenderMode", 1, 0]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemRGBg", 3, 255]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemSilhoHasError", 1, 0]
]
[RoutineCall,J_Dec1 ]

[RoutineDef, J_SilhouetteOn,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze, 
		[Loop,1,
			[VarSet, slSilhoHasError, [MVarGet, jmemSilhoHasError, 0]]
			[If, #slSilhoHasError == 1,
				[MVarSet, jmemSilhoHasError, 0, 0]
				[LoopExit]	
			,]
			[RoutineCall,J_CatchValidityError, slHasError ,slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			
			[Note, "Turning silhouette mode on. Please wait..."]
			[IHide,  1011] // Hides the Status bar
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas] 
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn] 
			
			[VarSet, slDocRange, [IGet, Document:Range]]
			[MVarSet, jmemDocRange, 0, #slDocRange]
			[ISet, Document:Range, 0]
			[MVaRSet, jmemPreviousColor, 0,  [IGet, Color:R]]
			[MVaRSet, jmemPreviousColor, 1,  [IGet, Color:G]]
			[MVaRSet, jmemPreviousColor, 2,  [IGet, Color:B]]
			
			[VarSet, slFloorStat, [IGetFlags, Draw:Floor]]
			[MVaRSet, jmemFloorStat, 0, #slFloorStat]
			[If, (#slFloorStat == 9 ) || (#slFloorStat == 11) || (#slFloorStat == 13) || (#slFloorStat == 1),
				[IUnPress, Draw:Floor ]
			,]
			
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IPress, Layer:Bake]
			[IPress, Layer:Clear] //Clear the canvas so the color of the center could be color picked.
			[MVarSet, jmemBGColor, 0, [PixolPick, 2, 0, 0]]
			[MVarSet, jmemBGColor, 1, [PixolPick, 3, 0, 0]]
			[MVarSet, jmemBGColor, 2, [PixolPick, 4, 0, 0]]
			
			//Need to insert drag the tool in the canvas
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			
			[VarSet, slSubToolCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
			[VarSet, slCtr, 0]
			[VarSet, slActiveSubtool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[Loop, #slSubToolCount,
				[SubToolSelect, #slCtr]
				[VarSet, slColorizeF, [IGetFlags, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
				[If, (#slColorizeF == 9) || (#slColorizeF == 11),
					[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 25]
					[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 50]
					[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 100]
				,
					[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 30]
					[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 60]
					[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 90]
				]
				[If, (#slColorizeF == 9) || (#slColorizeF == 11),
					[IUnPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize ]
				,]
				[VarInc, slCtr]
				[If,#slCtr == #slSubToolCount ,
					[IUpdate, 1]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
			]
			
			[SubToolSelect, #slActiveSubtool]
			[VarSet, slPreviewF, [IGetFlags, Render:Preview]]
			[VarSet, slFlatF, [IGetFlags, Render:Flat]]
			[VarSet, slFastF, [IGetFlags, Render:Fast]]
			[If, (#slPreviewF == 9) || (#slPreviewF == 11),
				[MVarSet, jmemCurrentRenderMode, 0, 1]
				
			,
				[If, (#slFlatF == 9) || (#slFlatF == 11),
					[MVarSet, jmemCurrentRenderMode, 0, 2]

				,
				
					[If, (#slFastF == 9) || (#slFastF == 11),
						[MVarSet, jmemCurrentRenderMode, 0, 3]

					,]
				]
			]
			[VarSet, slRenderMode,[MVarGet, jmemCurrentRenderMode, 0] ]
			[If, #slRenderMode== 2,
			
			,
				[IPress, Render:Fast]
			]
			[MVarSet, jmemRGBg, 0, [IGet, Color:R]]
			[MVarSet, jmemRGBg, 1, [IGet, Color:G]]
			[MVarSet, jmemRGBg, 2, [IGet, Color:B]]
			
			[ISet, Color:R, 255]
			[ISet, Color:G, 255]
			[ISet, Color:B, 255]
			[IPress, Document:Back]
			[ISet, Color:R, 0]
			[ISet, Color:G, 0]
			[ISet, Color:B, 0]
			
			[IShow,  1011] // Hides the Status bar 
			
		
		] //END OF LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			
			
		,]
	]//END OF IFREEZE
	[If, #slHasError == 1 ,
		[VarSet, gSiloHasError, 1]
		[IUnPress, ZScript:SilhouetteMode]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
]

[RoutineDef, J_SilhouetteOff,
	
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze, 
		[Loop,1,
			[VarSet, slSilhoHasError, [MVarGet, jmemSilhoHasError, 0]]
			[If, #slSilhoHasError == 1,
				[MVarSet, jmemSilhoHasError, 0, 0]
				[LoopExit]	
			,]
			
			[RoutineCall,J_CatchValidityError, slHasError ,slErrorToDisplay]
			
			//...continue
			
			[NOte, "Turning silhouette mode off. Please wait..."]
			[IHide,  1011] // Hides the Status bar
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas] 
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn] 
			
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			
			[VarSet, slSubToolCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
			[VarSet, slCtr, 0]
			[VarSet, slActiveSubtool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[IPress, Edit:UNDO]
			[VarSet, slSolo, -1]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloFlagsPressGet, slSolo]
			
			[Loop, #slSubToolCount,
				
				[SubToolSelect, #slCtr]
				
				[VarSet, slXRes, [IGet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res"]]
				[VarSet, slYRes, [IGet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res"]]
				[VarSet, slZRes, [IGet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res"]]
				[If, (#slXRes + #slYRes + #slZRes) == 175,
					[VarSet, slColorizeF, [IGetFlags, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
					[If, (#slColorizeF == 8) || (#slColorizeF == 10),
						[IPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize ]
					,]
				,
					[VarSet, slColorizeF, [IGetFlags, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
					[If, (#slColorizeF == 9) || (#slColorizeF == 11),
						[IUnPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize ]
					,]
				]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
				
				[VarInc, slCtr]
				[If, #slCtr == #slSubToolCount,
					[IUpdate, 1]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
			]
			
			[SubToolSelect, #slActiveSubtool]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloFlagsUnPress, slSolo]
			[VarSet, slCurrentRenderMode, [MVarGet, jmemCurrentRenderMode, 0]]
			[If, #slCurrentRenderMode == 1,
				[IPress,Render:Preview]
			,
				
				[If, #slCurrentRenderMode == 2,
					[IPress,Render:Flat] 
				,
					[If, #slCurrentRenderMode == 3,
						[IPress,Render:Fast] 
					,]
				]
			]
			
			[ISet, Color:R,  [MVarGet, jmemRGBg, 0]]
			[ISet, Color:G,  [MVarGet, jmemRGBg, 1]]
			[ISet, Color:B,  [MVarGet, jmemRGBg, 0]]
			[MVarSet, jmemRGBg, 0, 0]
			[MVarSet, jmemRGBg, 1, 0]
			[MVarSet, jmemRGBg, 2, 0]
					
			[ISet,Color:R, [MVarGet, jmemBGColor, 0]] 
			[ISet,Color:G, [MVarGet, jmemBGColor, 1]] 
			[ISet,Color:B, [MVarGet, jmemBGColor, 2]] 
			
 			[IPress, Document:Back]
 			[ISet, Document:Range, [MVarGet, jmemDocRange, 0]]
 			[ISet, Color:R, [MVarGet, jmemPreviousColor, 0]]
 			[ISet, Color:G, [MVarGet, jmemPreviousColor, 1]]
 			[ISet, Color:B, [MVarGet, jmemPreviousColor, 2]]
 			
 			[VarSet, slFloorStat, [MVarGet, jmemFloorStat, 0]]
 			[If, (#slFloorStat == 9 ) || (#slFloorStat == 11)  || (#slFloorStat == 13)  || (#slFloorStat == 1),
				[IPress, Draw:Floor ]
			,
				[IUnPress, Draw:Floor ]
			]
			[IShow,  1011] // Hides the Status bar 
			
		]// END OF LOOP
		
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			
			
		,]
	] // END OF IFREEZE
	[If, #slHasError == 1 ,
		[MVarSet, jmemSilhoHasError, 0, 1]
		[IPress, ZScript:SilhouetteMode]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
		
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
]
[ISwitch, SilhouetteMode, 0,"",
		[IKeyPress, '1',
			[RoutineCall, J_SilhouetteOn]
		]
	,
		[IKeyPress, '1',
			[RoutineCall, J_SilhouetteOff]
		]
		
		
]

[RoutineDef, J_DynaDec,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "memJ_DynameshTargetPoints", 1,250]
	[If, [MVarGet, memJ_DynameshTargetPoints, 0] < 4 ,
		[MVarSet, memJ_DynameshTargetPoints, 0, 250]
	,]
]

[RoutineCall, J_DynaDec]

[ISlider, "Target Points" , [MVarGet, memJ_DynameshTargetPoints, 0], 4, 10, 3000,,
	[RoutineCall, J_TargetPointsAdj]
,0, 200 ]

[RoutineDef, J_TargetPointsAdj,
	
	[MVarSet, memJ_DynameshTargetPoints, 0, [IGet, ZScript:Target Points]]
	
]

[Loop, 1,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemTargetPts", 1,0]
]

// ----------  POLYGROUPING BUTTON SECTION ------------------ 

[ISwitch, PGroups, 0, "", , ,0, 40]
// ----------  END OF POLYGROUPING BUTTON SECTION ------------------

[RoutineDef, J_MessageToggleDec,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet,"jmemDynaMessageToggle", 1,1]
]

[RoutineCall, J_MessageToggleDec]

[RoutineDef, J_DynaM,
	[IShowActions,0]	
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[VarSet, slPPaintFlags,[IGetFlags,Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
			[VarSet, slDrawFlags,[IGetFlags, "Transform:Draw Pointer"]]
			[VarSet, slMoveFlags,[IGetFlags, "Transform:Move"]]
			[VarSet, slScaleFlags,[IGetFlags, "Transform:Scale"]]
			[VarSet, slRotateFlags,[IGetFlags, "Transform:Rotate"]]
			[RoutineCall,J_CatchValidityError, slHasError ,slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[If, [MVArGet, jmemDynaMessageToggle, 0] == 1,
				[VarSet, slNoteFadeDuration, [IGet, Preferences:Interface:Notes Fade In-Out Time]]
				[ISet, Preferences:Interface:Notes Fade In-Out Time, 0]
				[Note, "Dynameshing, Please wait..." ]
				[ISet, Preferences:Interface:Notes Fade In-Out Time, 0]
			]
			[VarSet, slOpTime, [ZBrushInfo, 2]]
			[IHide,  1011] // Hides the Status bar
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 374, 629]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 375, 0,0,0,0]
			
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit] //Go outside edit mode.
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 383, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear] // So that when you mergeundo and also unmask first
			[VarSet, slPPaintStat,0]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slPPaintFlags/2)) > 0),
				[VarSet, slPPaintStat,1]
			]
			[VarSet, slDynaFlag, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:DynaMesh]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slDynaFlag/2)) > 0),
				[MergeUndo]
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:DynaMesh]// Unpresses first the DynaMesh button to prevent operation failure.
			]
			
			[MVarSet, jmemTargetPts, 0, ([IGet, "ZScript:Target Points"]*1000)]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 397, 0,0,0,0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[RoutineCall, J_BakeGeometry]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolys, -1]// The hidden polys count
			[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPolys, 0 ]
			[VarSet, slHasHiddenPoints, 0]
			[If, #slHiddenPolys > 0,
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Hidden"]
			]
			
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 408, 0,0,0,0]
			//Saves the settings of the DynaMesh Palette
			[VarSet, slPolishFlag, [IGetFlags,Tool:Geometry:D.Polish]]
			[VarSet, slProjectFlag, [IGetFlags,Tool:Geometry:Project]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slPolishFlag/2)) > 0), // Unpress the polish button
				[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:D.Polish"]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 415, 0,0,0,0]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Blur,2]
			[IModSet, Tool:Geometry:Blur,0]
			[ISet,Tool:Geometry:SubProjection,.6]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slProjectFlag/2)) > 0), // Unpress the project button
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Project]
			]
			
			// Saves the position and size of the mesh
			[VarSet, slGeoXPos, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:X Position"]]
			[VarSet, slGeoYPos, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Y Position"]]
			[VarSet, slGeoZPos, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Z Position"]]
			[VarSet, slGeoXSize, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
			[VarSet, slGeoYSize, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
			[VarSet, slGeoZSize, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 430, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slGeoXYZSize, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size"]] // Saves the global size  for later use.
			[VarSet, slXScaleMagnification, 0]
			[VarSet, slYScaleMagnification, 0]
			[VarSet, slZScaleMagnification, 0]
			[VarSet, slTargetRes, ([IGet, ZScript:Target Points]*1000)] // Computation of the target point count x 1000
			
			[VarSet, slXScaledTo2, 0]
			[VarSet, slYScaledTo2, 0]
			[VarSet, slZScaledTo2, 0]
			[VarSet, slMagnification, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 441, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slMagnification, ([IGet, "Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size"]/2)]
			[VarSet, slXScaleMagnificationTemp, #slGeoXSize]
			[VarSet, slYScaleMagnificationTemp, #slGeoYSize]
			[VarSet, slZScaleMagnificationTemp, #slGeoZSize]
			
			[MergeUndo]
   			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size", 2]
   			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 449, 0,0,0,0]
   			[VarSet, slXScaledTo2, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
   			[VarSet, slYScaledTo2, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
   			[VarSet, slZScaledTo2, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
   			[VarSet, slXScaleMagnificationTemp, (#slXScaleMagnificationTemp / #slXScaledTo2)]
   			[VarSet, slYScaleMagnificationTemp, (#slYScaleMagnificationTemp / #slYScaledTo2)]
   			[VarSet, slZScaleMagnificationTemp, (#slZScaleMagnificationTemp / #slZScaledTo2)]
   			
   			// Check if PGroups buttons is on
			[VarSet, slPGroupsStatus, [IGetFlags, ZScript:PGroups]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slPGroupsStatus/2)) > 0),
				[VarSet, slGeometryGroupsStatus, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Groups]]
				[If, ((FRAC(#slGeometryGroupsStatus/2)) == 0),
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Groups]
				]
			,
				[VarSet, slGeometryGroupsStatus, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Groups]]
				[If, ((FRAC(#slGeometryGroupsStatus/2)) > 0),
					[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Groups]
				]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 470, 0,0,0,0]		
			[VaRSet, slTheRes, 0] // Declaration of this local variable in this level to prevent any error just in case.
			[VarSet, slXPosO, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:X Position"]]
			[VarSet, slYPosO, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Y Position"]]
			[VarSet, slZPosO, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Z Position"]]
			[If, #slXPosO != 0 ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:X Position", 0]
			]
			
			[If, #slYPosO != 0 ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Y Position", 0]
			]
			[If,#slZPosO != 0 ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Z Position", 0]
			]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 490, 0,0,0,0]
			
			// Pre dynamization of the subtool
			
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Resolution, 64]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:DynaMesh]
			
			[VarSet, slToolInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
			[VarSet, slPolysId, [StrFind, "Polys=", slToolInfo]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolysId, [StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slToolInfo]]
			[VarSet, slResCounter, [StrExtract, #slToolInfo, (#slPolysId + 6), (#slHiddenPolysId - 2)]]
			[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 503, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slThePerc, (FRAC(#slResCounter) + 1)]
			[VarSet,slTargetRes, (#slTargetRes * #slThePerc)]
			[VarSet, slOldResCounter, #slResCounter]
			// New Formula
			[VarSet, slLogConstant4, .60205999132798]
			[VarSet, slLogConstant2, .301029995664]
			[VarSet, slTargetLog, (LOG10(#slTargetRes * .001))]
			[VarSet, slResLog, (LOG10(#slResCounter * .001))]
			[VarSet,slLogDiff,((#slResLog -#slTargetLog) / #slTargetLog)]
			[VarSet, slLogDist4,(#slLogDiff /#slLogConstant4 )]
			[VArSet, slBaseResLog,(LOG10(64))]
			[VArSet, slBaseResLog,(#slBaseResLog + (#slLogDist4*#slLogConstant2))]
			[VarSet, slTheResMult, (10^^ #slBaseResLog)]
			
			[VarSet, slTheRes, #slTheResMult]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 519, 0,0,0,0]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Resolution,  #slTheRes]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:DynaMesh]
			
			[VarSet, slProcCtr, 1]
			[VarSet, slToolInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
			[VarSet, slPolysId, [StrFind, "Polys=", #slToolInfo]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolysId, [StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slToolInfo]]
			[VarSet, slResCounter, [StrExtract, #slToolInfo, (#slPolysId + 6), (#slHiddenPolysId - 2)]]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 529, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slLimit, 3000]
			[VarSet,slLimitDist, (ABS( #slResCounter - #slTargetRes))]
			[If, #slLimitDist > #slLimit ,
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
				[VarSet, slDiffCtr, ((#slResCounter - #slTargetRes)/#slTargetRes) ]
				[VarSet, slTargetInc, (#slTargetRes + #slDiffCtr)]
				[VarSet, slTargetLog, (LOG10(#slTargetInc * .001))]
				[VarSet, slResLog, (LOG10(#slOldResCounter * .001))]
				[VarSet,slLogDiff,(#slTargetLog - #slResLog)]
				[VarSet, slLogDist4,(#slLogDiff /#slLogConstant4 )]
				[VArSet, slBaseResLog,(LOG10(64))]
				[VArSet, slBaseResLog,(#slBaseResLog + (#slLogDist4*#slLogConstant2))]
				[VarSet, slTheResMult, (10^^ #slBaseResLog)]
				[VarSet, slTheRes, #slTheResMult]
				[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Resolution,  #slTheRes ]

				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:DynaMesh]
 				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 552, 0,0,0,0]
			]
			
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[VarSet, slTempXPos, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:X Position"]]
			[VarSet, slTempYPos, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Y Position"]]
			[VarSet, slTempZPos, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Z Position"]]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 559, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slTemp1, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size"]]
			[VarSet, slTemp2, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
			[VarSet, slTemp3, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
			[VarSet, slTemp4, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
			
			[VarSet, slTempXYZSize, (#slTemp1 - 2)] // The new xyz - 2 
			[VarSet, slTempXSize, (#slTemp2 - #slXScaledTo2)] 
			[VarSet, slTempYSize, (#slTemp3 - #slYScaledTo2)]
			[VarSet, slTempZSize, (#slTemp4 -  #slZScaledTo2)]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 570, 0,0,0,0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:X Position,(#slGeoXPos+(#slTempXPos * #slXScaleMagnificationTemp))]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Y Position,(#slGeoYPos+(#slTempYPos * #slYScaleMagnificationTemp))]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Z Position,(#slGeoZPos+(#slTempZPos * #slZScaleMagnificationTemp))]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet,"Tool:Geometry:X Size", (#slGeoXSize + (#slTempXSize * #slXScaleMagnificationTemp))]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet,"Tool:Geometry:Y Size", (#slGeoYSize + (#slTempYSize * #slYScaleMagnificationTemp))]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet,"Tool:Geometry:Z Size", (#slGeoZSize + (#slTempZSize * #slZScaleMagnificationTemp))]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 583, 0,0,0,0]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 585, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			
			[VarSet, slUndoMax, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[VarSet, slLayersDelStat, [IGetStatus, Tool:Layers:Delete]]
			[If, #slLayersDelStat ,
				[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]
				[MergeUndo]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_DisableGeometry]
			[VarSet, slUndoMaxAfter, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[VarSet, slNewRes, (#slTheRes / #slMagnification)]
			[VarSet, slDynaMEshFlags, [IGEtFlags, Tool:Geometry:DynaMesh]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slDynaMEshFlags/2)) > 0),
				[If, #slUndoMaxAfter <= #slUndoMax,
					[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]
				,]
				[MergeUndo]
				[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Resolution, #slNewRes]// Set the Resolution setting so it could be manually dnameshed without the plugin's help.
			,
				[ISet, Tool:Geometry:Resolution, #slNewRes]// Set the Resolution setting so it could be manually dnameshed without the plugin's help.
			]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 612, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slUndoMaxAfter, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoMaxAfter > #slUndoMax,
				[MergeUndo]
			]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0] // Ignore the Dynamic option
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall,J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 628, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[If, #slPPaintStat ,
				[IPress,Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
			,
				[IUnPress,Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slDrawFlags/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, "Transform:Draw Pointer"]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMoveFlags/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, "Transform:Move"]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slScaleFlags/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, "Transform:Scale"]
			]
			[If,  ((FRAC(#slRotateFlags/2)) > 0),
				[IPRess, "Transform:Rotate"]
			]
			[IShow,  1011] //Show status bar
			
		]
	]
	[MVArSet, jmemDynaMessageToggle, 0, 1]
	
	[If, #slHasError,
		[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
		[NoteBar, ""]
		
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,
		[NoteBar, ""]
		
		[VarSet, slOpTime, ([ZBrushInfo, 2] - #slOpTime)] // Displays time of dynameshing
		[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
		// Check if PGroups buttons is on. This is a copy of lines of codes above, because after the dynamesh operation, the Groups switch button does not behave as expected.
		[VarSet, slPGroupsStatus, [IGetFlags, ZScript:PGroups]]
		[If, ((FRAC(#slPGroupsStatus/2)) > 0),
			[VarSet, slGeometryGroupsStatus, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Groups]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slGeometryGroupsStatus/2)) == 0),
				[IToggle, Tool:Geometry:Groups]
			]
		,
			[VarSet, slGeometryGroupsStatus, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Groups]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slGeometryGroupsStatus/2)) > 0),
				[IToggle, Tool:Geometry:Groups]
			]
		]
		
		[If, [MVArGet, jmemDynaMessageToggle, 0] == 1,
			[Note, [StrMerge,[StrMerge,"Dynamesh Operation Successful. Time is ", #slOpTime]," seconds."]]
		]
			
	]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
	
]
[IButton, DynaM, "Dynamesh with enough details",
	[IKeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_DynaM]
	]
	
]

//FRONT MASK ONLY
[RoutineDef, J_FrontMaskOnly,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slSuccessful, 0]
	
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay,0, 0, 1, 0, 1]
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slCurrentBrushInfo, [IGetInfo, "Brush:Current Brush"]] 
			[VarSet, slIMMID, [StrFind, "IMM", #slCurrentBrushInfo]]
			[VarSet, slInsertID, [StrFind, "Insert", #slCurrentBrushInfo]]
			[VarSet, slIsInsertBrush, 0]
			[If, #slIMMID > -1,
				[VarSet, slIsInsertBrush, 1]
			,]
			
			[If, #slInsertID > -1,
				[VarSet, slIsInsertBrush, 1]
			,]
			[If, #slIsInsertBrush == 1,
				[IPress, Brush:Standard]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ResetSleep]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreExtraCameraCanvas]
			[VarSet,slSymmetryFlags,[IGetFlags, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]]
			
			[If, (#slSymmetryFlags == 9) || (#slSymmetryFlags == 11)  || (#slSymmetryFlags == 13) || (#slSymmetryFlags == 1),
				[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
			,]
			
			[NoteBar, "Click the mask area to isolate." ]
			[VarSet, slSuccessful, 1]
		
		]
	]
	
	[If, #slHasError == 1,
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
	[If, #slSuccessful,
		//[RoutineCall, J_StoreExtraCameraCanvas]
		//[RoutineCall, J_RegisterOnMeshSecondaryRoutine, "J_FrontRotateOffMesh"]
		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEDOWN, "J_FrontOnlyClick", 0, 1, 1]
	,]
]

// [RoutineDef, J_FrontRotateOffMesh,
// 	[Loop,1,
// 		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEvent, #LMOUSEUP, "J_FrontRotateOffMeshUnPress"]
// 	]
// ]
// 
// [RoutineDef, J_FrontRotateOffMeshUnPress,
// 	[IShowActions,0]
// 	[VarSet, slSuccessful, 0]
// 	[Loop,1,
// 		[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
// 		[VArSet, slSuccessful, 1]
// 	]
// 	[IShowActions,1]
// 	[IUpdate, 1,1]
// 	[If, #slSuccessful,
// 		[RoutineCall, J_StoreExtraCameraCanvas]
// 		[RoutineCall, J_RegisterOnMeshSecondaryRoutine, "J_FrontRotateOffMesh"]
// 		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEDOWN, "J_FrontOnlyClick", 0, 1, 1]
// 	,]
// ]

[RoutineDef, J_FrontOnlyClick,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[VarSet, slSuccessful, 0]
	[VarSet, slNoteMessage, "Evaluating clicked mask.Please wait."]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[Varset, slMouseH, [MouseHPos]]//Gets the mouse X position
			[Varset, slMouseV, [MouseVPos]]//Gets the mouse Y position
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 0, 0, 1, 0, 1]
			//...continue
			[Note, #slNoteMessage] // The overlay message.
			
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			
			[VarSet, slMaxUndoHistory, [IGet, "Preferences:Undo History:Max Undo History"]]
			[ISet, "Preferences:Undo History:Max Undo History", 10000]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[VarSet, slIsInTransformationBox, 0]
			[VarSet, slCanvasWindowWidth,[IWidth, 1004]]
			[VarSet, slCanvasWindowHeight,[IHeight, 1004]]
			[VarSet, slCanvasWidth, [IGet, "Document:Width"]]
			[VarSet, slCanvasHeight, [IGet, "Document:Height"]]
			[VarSet, slPanH, [CanvasPangetH]]
			[VarSet, slPanV, [CanvasPangetV]]
			[VarSet, slZoomFact, [CanvasZoomGet]]
			[VarSet, slWinMultH, ((((#slCanvasWindowWidth * .5)-38)/#slZoomFact))]
			[VarSet, slWinMultV, ((((#slCanvasWindowHeight * .5)-38)/#slZoomFact))]
			[VarSet, slTop, (#slPanV-#slWinMultV)] 
			[If, #slTop < 0 ,
				[VarSet, slTop, 0] 
			,]
			[VarSet, slLeft, (#slPanH-#slWinMultH)] 
			[If, #slLeft < 0 ,
				[VarSet, slLeft, 0] 
			,]
			[VarSet, slBottom, (#slPanV+#slWinMultV)] 
			[If, #slBottom > #slCanvasHeight ,
				[VarSet, slBottom, #slCanvasHeight] 
			,]
			[VarSet, slRight, (#slPanH+#slWinMultH)] 
			[If, #slRight > #slCanvasWidth ,
				[VarSet, slRight, #slCanvasWidth] 
			,]
			[VArSet, slOffsetH,0]
			[VArSet, slOffsetV,0]
			[If, (#slMouseH <= #slLeft) &&  (#slLeft >= 0) ,
				[VarSet, slOffsetH, (-38/#slZoomFact)]
			,]
			[If, (#slMouseH >= #slRight) &&  (#slRight <= #slCanvasWidth) ,
				[VarSet, slOffsetH, (38/#slZoomFact)]
			,]
			[If, (#slMouseV <= #slTop) &&  (#slTop >= 0) ,
				
				[VarSet, slOffsetV, (-38/#slZoomFact)]
			,]
			[If, (#slMouseV >= #slBottom) &&  (#slBottom <= #slCanvasHeight) ,
				[VarSet, slOffsetV, (38/#slZoomFact)]
			,]
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[VarSet, slDepthClicked, [PixolPick, 1,#slMouseH,#slMouseV ]]
			
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[VArSet, slHasLayers, 0]
			[VArSet, slHasLayersFlags, [IGetStatus,"Tool:Layers:Delete"]]
			[If, #slHasLayersFlags,
				[VArSet, slHasLayers, 1]
			,]
			[VarSet,sliSSoftMask, 0]
			[VarSet, slMaskReset, [IExists,"Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Reset"]]
			[If, #slMaskReset == 0 ,
				[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile"]
				
			,]
			[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Reset"]
			[VarSet, slPreUndo, [Iget, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Focal Shift", -70]
			[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Blur", 0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Strength", 2]
			[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Apply"]
			[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Reset"]
			[VarSet, slPostUndo, [Iget, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slPostUndo > #slPreUndo ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[VarSet,sliSSoftMask, 1]
				
			,]
			[If, #slMaskReset == 0,
				[IExists,"Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Close"]
			,]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPoints, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
			[If, #slHiddenPoints > 0 ,
				[IPress, Tool:Visibility:ShowPt]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPointsBefore, #slHiddenPoints]
			[IPress, Tool:Visibility:HidePt] // Hide the points. Hide the outline
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[VarSet, slDepthClicked2, [PixolPick, 1,#slMouseH,#slMouseV ]]
			[VarSet, slDepthDiff, (ABS(#slDepthClicked2 - #slDepthClicked))]
			[If, #slDepthDiff <=.0008, // This means you clicked the masked area
			,
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Clear"]
				[IPress,Edit:Tool:Undo]
				[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slSuccessful, 0]
				[IPress, Transform:Edit]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must clicked on a masked area. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit] 
			]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPoints, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
			[If, slHiddenPointsBefore == slHiddenPoints,
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slSuccessful, 0]
				[IPress, Transform:Edit]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "There is no mask to fill. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit] 
			,]
			//..continue
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]// Clear the masks of the mesh
			[VarSet, slSymmetryFlags, [IGetFlags, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]]
			[VarSet,slHasSymmetry,0]
			[If, (#slSymmetryFlags == 9) || (#slSymmetryFlags == 11) || (#slSymmetryFlags == 13) || (#slSymmetryFlags == 1),
				[VarSet,slHasSymmetry,1]
			,]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 4363,4458]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4364, 0,0,0,0]
			//Set the currentbrush to Standard then make the stroke to DragRect
			[VarSet, slShiftCtrlBrush, ""]
			[VarSet, slTempA, 0]

[ISlider, GrowMask , 0, 1, -100, 100,,
	[IKeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_GrowMaskFunc]
	]
, 0, 100 ]

[RoutineDef, J_GrowMaskFunc,
	[VarSet, slAdjust, 1]
	[If, #slAdjust ,
		[RoutineCall, J_GrowMask]
		[VarSet, slAdjust, 0]
	,]
	[If, #slAdjust == 0,
		[ISet, "ZScript:GrowMask", 0]
	,]
	
]

[RoutineDef, J_GrowMask,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VArSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			
			
			[VarSet, slGrowVal, [IGet, "ZScript:GrowMask"]]
			[VarSet, slCtr, 0]
			[If, (#slGrowVal > 0) || (#slGrowVal < 0),
				[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
				[VarSet, slIsPositive, 0]
				[If, #slGrowVal > 0 ,
					[VarSet, slIsPositive, 1]
					[Note, "Growing masks. Please wait..."]
				,]
				[If, #slGrowVal < 1 ,
					[VarSet, slIsPositive, 0]
					[Note, "Shrinking masks. Please wait..."]
				,]
				[IHide, 1011]
				[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
				[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
				[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
				[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
				[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 4813,4866]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4814, 0,0,0,0]
				[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
				[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
				[VarSet, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[If, #slHiddenPoints > 0 ,
					[IPress, Tool:Visibility:ShowPt]
					[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
					[MergeUndo]
				,]
				[VarSet, slHiddenPointsBefore, #slHiddenPoints]
				[IPress, Tool:Visibility:HidePt] // Hide the points
				[VarSet, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[If, slHiddenPointsBefore == slHiddenPoints,
					[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
					[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
					[IPress, Transform:Edit]
					[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
					[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "There is no mask to evaluate. Action cancelled."]
					[LoopExit] 
				,]
				//..continue
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[VarSet, slGrowVal, ABS(#slGrowVal)]
				[Loop, #slGrowVal,
					[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4820, #slGrowVal,#slCtr,4820,4859]
					[If, #slIsPositive ,
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile"]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Reset"]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Blur", 1]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Focal Shift", -50]
						[If, #slCtr > 0 ,
							[MergeUndo]
						,]
					,
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile"]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Reset"]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Blur", 1]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Focal Shift", 30]
						[If, #slCtr > 0 ,
							[MergeUndo]
						,]
						
					]
					
					[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Apply"]
					[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4841, #slGrowVal,#slCtr,4820,4859]
					[If, #slIsPositive ,
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:fV"]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Focal Shift", 30]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Blur", 0]
						[MergeUndo]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Apply"]
						[MergeUndo]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Apply"]
					,
						
					
					]
					[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4854, #slGrowVal,#slCtr,4820,4859]
					[VarInc, slCtr ]
					[If, #slCtr == #slGrowVal,
						[IUpdate, 1]
						[LoopExit]
					,]
				]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4861, 0, 0, 0, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
				[IPress, Transform:Edit]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
				[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4866, 0, 0, 0, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			,]
		]
	]
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NOte, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]



[ISlider, MaskSharpness , 0, 1, -100, 100,,
	[IkeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall,J_MaskSharpnessFunc ]
	]
, 0, 100 ]

[RoutineDef, J_MaskSharpnessFunc,
	[VarSet, slAdjust, 1]
	[If, #slAdjust ,
		[RoutineCall, J_MaskSharpness]
		[VarSet, slAdjust, 0]
	,]
	[If, #slAdjust == 0,
		[ISet, "ZScript:MaskSharpness", 0]
	,]

]


[RoutineDef, J_MaskSharpness,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VArSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slSharpnessVal, [IGet, "ZScript:MaskSharpness"]]
			[VarSet, slCtr, 0]
			[If, (#slSharpnessVal > 0) || (#slSharpnessVal < 0),
				[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
				//..continue
				
				[VarSet, slIsSharp, 0]
				[If, #slSharpnessVal > 0 ,
					[VarSet, slIsSharp, 1]
					[Note, "Sharpening masks. Please wait..."]
				,]
				[If, #slSharpnessVal < 1 ,
					[VarSet, slIsSharp, 0]
					[Note, "Blurring masks. Please wait..."]
				,]
				
				[IHide, 1011]
				[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
				[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
				[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
				[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
				[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 4929,4979]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4930, 0,0,0,0]
				[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
				[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
				[VarSet, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[If, #slHiddenPoints > 0 ,
					[IPress, Tool:Visibility:ShowPt]
					[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
					[MergeUndo]
				,]
				[VarSet, slHiddenPointsBefore, #slHiddenPoints]
				[IPress, Tool:Visibility:HidePt] // Hide the points
				[VarSet, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
				[If, slHiddenPointsBefore == slHiddenPoints,
					[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
					[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
					[IPress, Transform:Edit]
					[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
					[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "There is no mask to evaluate. Action cancelled."]
					[LoopExit] 
				,]
				
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				
				[VarSet, slSharpnessVal, ABS(#slSharpnessVal)]
				
				[Loop, #slSharpnessVal,
					[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4930,#slSharpnessVal,#slCtr,4937,4970]
					[If, #slIsSharp,
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile"]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Reset"]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:fV"]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Blur", 0]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Focal Shift", 30]
						[If, #slCtr > 0 ,
							[MergeUndo]
						,]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Apply"]
						[MergeUndo]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Apply"]
					,
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile"]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:Reset"]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Mask Adjust Profile:fV"]
						[ISet, "Tool:Masking:Blur", 1]
						[If, #slCtr > 0 ,
							[MergeUndo]
						,]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Apply"]
						[MergeUndo]
						[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Apply"]
					]
					[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4962,#slSharpnessVal,#slCtr,4937,4970]
					
					
					
					[VarInc, slCtr ]
					[If, #slCtr == #slSharpnessVal,
						[IUpdate, 1]
						[LoopExit]
					,]
				]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4972, 0, 0, 0, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
				
				[IPress, Transform:Edit]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
				[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 4978, 0, 0, 0, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			,]
		]
	]
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NOte, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]
]

[RoutineDef, J_MaskBrushHardSize,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VArSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slMaskMode, [MVarGet,jmemMaskBrushMode, 0]]
			[If, #slMaskMode == 0 ,
				
				[IKeyPress, CTRL,
					
					[IPress, "Brush:Tablet Pressure:Size"]
					
					[VarSet,slFolderName,"MaskCurves"]
					[VarSet,slFolderName,[FileNameResolvePath,"MaskCurves"]]
					
					//check to see if folder exists
					[VarSet,slFolderExists,[FileExecute,[Var,gFileUtilsPath],"FolderExists",#slFolderName]]
					[If,#slFolderExists,
					 ,//else
						[VArSet, slHasError, 1]
						[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, [StrMerge, #slFolderName," folder is missing. Action cancelled."]]
						[LoopExit]
					 ]
					[VarSet, slFileName, [StrMerge, #slFolderName,"/MaskSizeCurve.zcv"]]
					[VarSet, slFileName, [FileNameResolvePath, #slFileName]]
					 [VarSet, slFileExists,#slFileName ]
					 [If, #slFileExists ,
					 	[VarSet, slErr, [FileExecute, [Var,gFileUtilsPath],"FileDelete",#slFileName]] 
					 ,]
					[FileNameSetNext, #slFileName]
					[IPress,"Brush:Tablet Pressure:Size:Save" ]
					 
					[VarSet, slSizeFocal, [IGet,"Brush:Tablet Pressure:Size:Focal Shift"]]
					[MVarSet, jmemMaskBrushSetting, 0, #slSizeFocal]
					[VarSet, slSizeOffset, [IGet,"Brush:Tablet Pressure:Size:Offset"]]
					[MVarSet, jmemMaskBrushSetting, 1, #slSizeOffset]
					[VarSet, slSizeNoise, [IGet,"Brush:Tablet Pressure:Size:Noise"]]
					[MVarSet, jmemMaskBrushSetting, 2, #slSizeNoise]
					[VarSet, slSizeTile, [IGet,"Brush:Tablet Pressure:Size:Tile"]]
					[MVarSet, jmemMaskBrushSetting, 3, #slSizeTile]
					[VarSet, slSizeStrength, [IGet,"Brush:Tablet Pressure:Size:Strength"]]
					[MVarSet, jmemMaskBrushSetting, 4, #slSizeStrength]
					[IPress, "Brush:Tablet Pressure:RGB Intensity"]
					[VarSet, slFileName, [StrMerge, #slFolderName,"/MaskRGBCurve.zcv"]]
					[VarSet, slFileName, [FileNameResolvePath, #slFileName]]
					 [VarSet, slFileExists,#slFileName ]
					 [If, #slFileExists ,
					 	[VarSet, slErr, [FileExecute, [Var,gFileUtilsPath],"FileDelete",#slFileName]] 
					 ,]
					[FileNameSetNext, #slFileName]
