[RoutineDef, J_DuplicatePolyMesh,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay,1, 1, 0, 0, 1]
			//...Continue
			[Note, "Duplicating polys. Please wait..."]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[RoutineCall, J_FixGhostingCrash]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 5256,5284 ]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5257, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Inverse] // Inverse mask
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5261, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Visibility:HidePt] // hide
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5266, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Polygroups:GroupVisible]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5269, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy] 
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5272, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:MaskAll]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Append"]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5276, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5278, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IPress, Transform:Move]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5281, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[IPress, "Transform:Gizmo 3D"]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetGizmoUnmasked]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 5284, 0, 0 , 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
		]// END OF LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
			
		,]
		
	]
	
	[If, #slHasError == 1,
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
	
]
[IButton, DuplicatePolyMesh, "",
	[IKeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_DuplicatePolyMesh]
	]
	
	
]

[RoutineDef, J_RemeshAdaptDec,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jMemRemeshAdapt", 1,1]
	
	[If, [MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0] < 0 ,
		[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0, 1]
	,]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jMemRemeshAlternate", 1,0]
	
	[If, [MVarGet, jMemRemeshAlternate, 0] < 0 ,
		[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAlternate, 0, 0]
	,]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jMemHighestLevelSubD", 1,4]
	[If, [MVarGet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0] < 1 ,
		[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0, 4]
	,]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jMemLowestLevel", 1,4.5]
	[If, [MVarGet, jMemLowestLevel, 0] < 4.5 ,
		[MVarSet, jMemLowestLevel, 0, 4.5]
	,]
	
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jMemHighestLevel", 1,288]
	[VarSet, slTCtr, ([MVarGet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0] - 1)]
	[VarSet, slPolyCount, [MVarGet, jMemLowestLevel, 0]]
	[Loop, #slTCtr,
		[VarSet,slPolyCount,(#slPolyCount * 4)]
	]
	
	[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevel, 0, #slPolyCount]
	
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jMemRemeshAdjusted", 1,0]

]

[RoutineCall, J_RemeshAdaptDec]

[ISwitch, AdaptRes, [MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0],"", 
	[RoutineCall, J_AdaptResPress]
,	
	[RoutineCall, J_AdaptResUnPress]
]
[RoutineDef, J_AdaptResPress,
	[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0, 1]
	[IDisable, "ZScript:Lowest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]
	[IDisable, "ZScript:Highest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]
	[IDisable, "ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl"]

]
[RoutineDef, J_AdaptResUnPress,
	[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0, 0]
	[IEnable, "ZScript:Lowest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]
	[IEnable, "ZScript:Highest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]
	[IEnable, "ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl"]

]

[ISlider, "Lowest Lvl Polys 1=1k", [MVarGet, jMemLowestLevel, 0], .01, 4.5,50, "",
	
	[RoutineCall, J_AdjustLowestLvlPoly]
	
, [MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0],  160]

[ISlider, "Highest Lvl Polys 1=1k", [MVarGet, jMemHighestLevel, 0], .01, 18, 3000, "", 
	
	[RoutineCall, J_AdjustHighestLvlPoly]
,[MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0], 170]

[ISlider, "Highest SubD Lvl", [MVarGet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0], 1, 1, 10, "", 
	[RoutineCall, J_AdjustHighestLvl]
,[MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdapt, 0], 130]


[RoutineDef, J_AdjustLowestLvlPoly,
	
	[If, [MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0] == 0 ,
		[Loop, 1,
			[VarSet,slTheLowestVal , [IGet,"ZScript:Lowest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]]
			[MVarSet, jMemLowestLevel, 0, #slTheLowestVal]
			//Adjusting this slider will automatically adjusts the highest poly slider
			[VarSet, slSubDlvl,[IGet,"ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl"]]
			[VarSet, slCtr, 0]
			[VarSet,slLimit, 3000]
			[Loop,(#slSubDlvl - 1),
				[VarSet, slTempCtr,(#slTheLowestVal * 4) ]
				
				[If, #slTempCtr > #slLimit,
					[LoopExit]
				,]
				[VarSet,slTheLowestVal, #slTempCtr]
				[VarInc, slCtr]
			]
			[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0,1]
			[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevel, 0, #slTheLowestVal ]
			[ISet, "ZScript:Highest Lvl Polys 1=1k", #slTheLowestVal]
			[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0,(#slCtr + 1)]
			[ISet, "ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl", (#slCtr + 1)]
			[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0,0]
		]

	,]
	
]

[RoutineDef, J_AdjustHighestLvlPoly,
	[If, [MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0] == 0 ,
		[IShowActions,0]
		[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
		[VArSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
		
		[Loop, 1,
			[VarSet,slTheHighestVal , [IGet,"ZScript:Highest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]]
			[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevel, 0, #slTheHighestVal]
			[VarSet,slLowestPoly, [MVarGet, jMemLowestLevel, 0]]
			[VarSet, slLowestLimit,18]
			[VarSet,slTheCurrentVal, #slTheHighestVal]
			[VArSet,slCtr, 0]
			[Loop, 100,
				[If, #slTheCurrentVal < #slLowestLimit ,
					[LoopExit]
				,]
				[VarSet,slTheCurrentVal, (#slTheCurrentVal / 4)]
				[VarInc, slCtr]
				[If, #slCtr>=100 ,
					[IUpdate,1]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
			]
			[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0,1]
			[MVarSet, jMemLowestLevel, 0, #slTheCurrentVal ]
			[ISet, "ZScript:Lowest Lvl Polys 1=1k", #slTheCurrentVal]
			[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0,(#slCtr + 1)]
			[ISet, "ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl", (#slCtr + 1)]
			[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0,0]
		]
		[If, #slHasError ,
			[NOte,#slErrorToDisplay]
		,]
		[IShowActions,1]
	,]
	

]

[RoutineDef, J_AdjustHighestLvl,
	[If, [MVarGet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0] == 0 ,
		[IShowActions,0]
		[VarSet, slHasError,0]
		[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay,""]
		[Loop, 1,
			[VArSet, slTheLimit,3000]
			[VarSet,slTheHighestLvl , [IGet,"ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl"]]
			[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0, #slTheHighestLvl]
			[VarSet, slLowestLvlPoly,[MVarGet, jMemLowestLevel, 0]]
			[VArSet, slCtr, 0]
			[VArSet, slLoopCtr, ([MVarGet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0] - 1)]
			[VArSet, slFinalHighCount, [MVarGet, jMemLowestLevel, 0]]
			[VarSet, slHasReachedLimit,0]
			[VarSet, slSubDCtr, 1]
			[Loop, #slLoopCtr,
				[VarSet,slTemp, (#slFinalHighCount * 4)]
				
				[If, #slTemp > #slTheLimit ,
					[VArSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VArSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Polycount of the Highest Level has exceeded the 3 million limit. The slider cannot be adjusted."]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
				[VArSet, slFinalHighCount, (#slFinalHighCount * 4)]
				[VarInc, slSubDCtr]
			]
			
			[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0,1]
			[ISet, "ZScript:Highest Lvl Polys 1=1k", #slFinalHighCount]
			[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevel, 0, #slFinalHighCount ]
			[ISet,"ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl", #slSubDCtr]
			[MVarSet, jMemHighestLevelSubD, 0,#slSubDCtr]
			[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAdjusted, 0,0]
		]
		[If, #slHasError ,
			[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
		,]
		[IShowActions,1]
	,]
	
]

// ************* REMESHING TOOLS ********************
[ISwitch, AltRemeshing,[MVarGet, jMemRemeshAlternate, 0],"",
	[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAlternate, 0, 1]
, 
	[MVarSet, jMemRemeshAlternate, 0, 0]
,0, 80]

[RoutineDef, J_RemeshQ,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[VarSet, slMrgbFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:Mrgb]]
	[VarSet, slRgbFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:Rgb]]
	[VarSet, slMFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:M]]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
				
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay,1, 1, 1, 1, 1]
			//...continue
			
			[NOTE, "Please wait..."]
			[VarSet, slAltPressed, 0]
			[VarSet, slAltFlags, [IgetFlags, "ZScript:AltRemeshing"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slAltFlags / 2)) > 0), [VarSet, slAltPressed, 1]]
			
			[VarSet, slCurvDelStats, [IsDisabled, Stroke:Delete]]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slAdaptResFlags,[IGetFlags,"ZScript:AdaptRes"]]
			[VarSet, slAdaptRes, 0]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slAdaptResFlags / 2)) > 0), [VarSet, slAdaptRes, 1]]
			
			[IPress,Draw:Mrgb]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			//Get the area of the bounding box
			[VArSet, slBoxWidthBefore, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxHeightBefore, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxLengthBefore, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaBefore, (#slBoxWidthBefore * #slBoxHeightBefore * #slBoxLengthBefore)]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[VarSet, slToolIndex, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]	//Save first the index of the active ztool
			//Check if thereis subdiv then bake
			[VArSet, slDynamicFlags,[IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]]
			[VArSet,slHasSDynamic, 0]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slDynamicFlags / 2)) > 0), [VArSet,slHasSDynamic, 1]]
			
			[If, #slHasSDynamic ,
				[If, [MVarGet, jmemSDivNoteToggle,0] == 1 ,
					[Ipress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Apply"]
					[MergeUndo]
					[VarSet,slDelLowerStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
					[If, #slDelLowerStat ,  [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
				,
					[VarSet,slFlatDiv,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:s.FlatSubdiv"]]
					[VarSet,slSmtDiv,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:s.SmoothSubdiv"]]
					[VarSet, slDivCnt, #slFlatDiv]
					[If, #slSmtDiv > #slFlatDiv , [VarSet, slDivCnt, #slSmtDiv]]
					
					// Get the points of the mesh
					[VarSet, slMeshInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
					[VarSet, slPointsId, [StrFind, "Points=", #slMeshInfo]]
					[VarSet, slHiddenPolys,[StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slMeshInfo]]
					[VarSet, slOrigPoints, [StrExtract, slMeshInfo, (#slPointsId + 7), (#slHiddenPolys - 2)]]
					[VarSet,slPointsLog,(LOG10(#slOrigPoints * .001))]
					[VArSet, slLogConst,(.60205999132798 * #slDivCnt )]
					[VarSet, slSubDivPoints,(10^^(#slPointsLog + #slLogConst))]
					
					[VArSet,slTheRoundedPoints, 0]
					[VarSet,slTheMessage, ""]
					[If, #slSubDivPoints >= 1000 ,
						[VArSet,slTheRoundedPoints, (#slSubDivPoints * .001)]
						[If, #slSubDivPoints <= 3000 ,
							[VarSet,slTheMessage,[StrMerge,"The output polys will be around ", #slTheRoundedPoints, " million because Dynamic Subdivision is on. Would you like it to be turned off before proceeding?"]]
							[NoteIButton, "OK"]
							[NoteIButton, "NO"]
							[NoteIButton, "Always OK(skip this note until bext restart)"]
						,
							[VarSet,slTheMessage,"The output polys will be more than 3 million because Dynamic Subdivision is on. Action is going to be canceled."]
							[NoteIButton, "OK"]
						]
						
					,
						[VArSet,slTheRoundedPoints, (#slSubDivPoints * 1000)]
						[VarSet,slTheMessage,[StrMerge,"The output polys will be around ", #slTheRoundedPoints, " because Dynamic Subdivision is on. Would you like it to be turned off before proceeding?"]]
						[NoteIButton, "OK"]
						[NoteIButton, "NO"]
						[NoteIButton, "Always OK(skip this note until bext restart)"]
					]
					
					
					[VarSet, slDynamicNote,[NOte, #slTheMessage]]
					[If, #slDynamicNote == 1 ,
						[If, #slSubDivPoints > 3000,
							[VaRSet, slHasError, 1]
							[LoopExit]
						,
							[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]
						]
					]
					
					[If,  #slDynamicNote == 2 ,
						[Ipress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Apply"]
						[MergeUndo]
						[VarSet,slDelLowerStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
						[If, #slDelLowerStat , [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
					]
					
					[If,  #slDynamicNote == 3 ,
						[MVarSet, jmemSDivNoteToggle,0, 1]
						[Ipress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Apply"]
						[MergeUndo]
						[VarSet,slDelLowerStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
						[If, #slDelLowerStat , [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
					]
				]
			]
			
			[IPress, Tool:Clone]
			[VarSet, slToolLast, ([ToolGetCount] - 1)]
			[ToolSelect, #slToolLast] // Select the clone
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[VarSet, slXYZSize, [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size"]]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size", 2]
			//Check for any symmetry
			
			[VarSet, slSymmetryOn, 0]
			[VarSet, slXMirFlags, [IGetFlags, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slXMirFlags / 2)) > 0),
				[VarSet, slSymmetryOn, 1]
				[IModSet, "Tool:SubTool:ReMesh All", 1]
			,
				[IModSet, "Tool:SubTool:ReMesh All", 0]
			]
			
			[IPress, Edit:Tool:DelUH]
			
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Polish, 10]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Res, 64]
			[IModSet, Tool:SubTool:Polish, 1]
			//..Continue
			
			// Get the points of the mesh
			[VarSet, slMeshInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
			[VarSet, slPointsId, [StrFind, "Points=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolys,[StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slOrigPoints, [StrExtract, slMeshInfo, (#slPointsId + 7), (#slHiddenPolys - 2)]]
				
			[VarSet, slLogConstant, .60205999132798]
			[VarSet, slOrigPointsLog, (LOG10(#slOrigPoints * .001))]
			
			[VarSet,slSubDivCount,((INT(#slOrigPointsLog / #slLogConstant)) - 1)]
			
			[VarSet, slConstantMult,(#slLogConstant * #slSubDivCount)]
			[VarSEt, slTheBase, (10 ^^ (#slOrigPointsLog- #slConstantMult))]
			[VarSet, slTargetCount, 0]
			
			[If, #slAltPressed ,
				[Note, "Step 1 - Please wait... Now remeshing (ALT)..."]
			,
				[Note, "Step 1 - Please wait... Now remeshing..."]
			]
			
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:ReMesh All"]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size", #slXYZSize ]
			[VArSet, slScaleFact, (#slXYZSize/2)]
			[SubToolSelect, ([SubToolGetCount] - 1)]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:XYZ Size", (#slScaleFact*2)]
			[IPress,Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			
			[ToolSelect, #slToolIndex]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[If, #slHasSDynamic ,
				[IUnPress,  "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]
				[MergeUndo]
			]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			
			// Check if there are generated polygroups
			[VarSet, slHasPolyGorups, 0]
			[VarSet, slMaskingBorder, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingBorder / 2)) > 0), [IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			
			[VarSet, slMaskingCrease, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingCrease / 2)) > 0), [IUnPress,  "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[VarSet, slMaskingGroups, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingGroups / 2)) == 0), [IPress, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			
			[VarSet, slUndoStep, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			
			[IPress,"Tool:Masking:MaskByFeature"]
			[VarSet, slUndoStepAfter, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoStepAfter >  #slUndoStep,
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
				[VarSet, slHasPolyGorups, 1]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingBorder / 2)) > 0), [IPress, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingCrease / 2)) > 0), [IPress,  "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingGroups / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			
			[If, #slAdaptRes ,
				[If, #slSymmetryOn ,
					[If, #slAltPressed ,
						[VarSet, slTargetCount, (#slTheBase)]
					,
						[VarSet, slTargetCount, (#slTheBase*.5)]
					]
					
				,
					[VarSet, slTargetCount, #slTheBase]
				]
				
			,
				[VarSet, slTargetLowest,[IGet, "ZScript:Lowest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]]
				[VarSet, slTargetCount, #slTargetLowest]
				
				[If, #slSymmetryOn ,
					[If, !#slAltPressed , [VarSet, slTargetCount, (#slTargetCount*.5)]]
				]
			
			]
			
			[If, #slSymmetryOn,
				[IPress, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]
				[If, #slAltPressed,
					[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", (#slTargetCount)]
				,
					[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", #slTargetCount]
				]
			,
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", #slTargetCount]
			]
			
			[VarSet, slKeepGrpFlags, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups"]]
			// ZREMESH
			[If, #slHasPolyGorups,
				[If, ((FRAC(#slKeepGrpFlags / 2)) == 0), [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups"]]
			,
				[If, ((FRAC(#slKeepGrpFlags / 2)) > 0), [IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups"]]
			]
			
			[VArSet, slHasCurves,0]
			[If, #slCurvDelStats == 0, [VArSet, slHasCurves,1]]
			[VarSet, slFreezeBorderFlags, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder]]
			[VarSet, slFreezeGroupsFlags, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups]]
			[VarSet, slUsePPFlags, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]]
			[VarSet, slGeoHalf, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Half]]
			[VarSet, slGeoDouble, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Double]]
			[VarSet, slGeoSame, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Same]]
			[VarSet, slGeoAdapt, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]]
			[VarSet,slAdaptSizePrior,[IGet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize]]
			[VarSet,slCurveStr,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength"]]
			[VarSet,slSmoothSet,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups"]]
			[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder]
			[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups]
			[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Half]
			[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Double]
			[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Same]
			[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize, 50]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups", 1]
			[If, #slHasCurves ,
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 100]
			,
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 0]
			]
			
			[MergeUndo]
			[If, #slAltPressed,
				[IKeyPress, ALT,
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
				]
			
			,
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
			]
			// Get the points of the mesh and remesh until it gets the right target
			[VarSet, slMeshInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
			[VarSet, slPointsId, [StrFind, "Points=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolys,[StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slPoints, [StrExtract, #slMeshInfo, (#slPointsId + 7), (#slHiddenPolys - 2)]]
			
			[VArSet, slPointsLog, (LOG10(#slPoints * .001))]
			[VarSet, slConstantMult,(#slLogConstant * #slSubDivCount)]
			[VarSet,slHighestSubDLog, (#slPointsLog + #slConstantMult)]
			[VarSet,slFinalSubDCount, ((10 ^^ #slHighestSubDLog) * 1000)]
			[VarSet, slPointsDiff, (ABS(#slFinalSubDCount - #slOrigPoints ))]
			[VarSet, slLimit,3000]
			[If, #slPointsDiff >= #slLimit , [VarSet, slRemeshAgain, 1]]

			[If,#slRemeshAgain == 1,
				
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[If, #slHasSDynamic ,
					[IUnPress,  "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]
					[MergeUndo]
				]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
				[VarSet, slOrigLog,(LOG10(#slOrigPoints * .001))]
				[VarSet, slOrigSubD,(LOG10(#slFinalSubDCount * .001))]
				[VarSet, slLeftSide, ((#slOrigSubD -#slOrigLog)/#slOrigLog )]
				[VarSet, slZRemRes, (1.1*(#slTargetCount + #slLeftSide))]
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", #slZRemRes]
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]
				[ISet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize, 50]
				[If, #slHasCurves,
					[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 100]
				,
					[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 0]
				]
				
				[MergeUndo]
				
				[If, #slAltPressed,
					[IKeyPress, ALT,
						[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
					]
				
				,
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
				]
				
			]
			
			//Reset the settings
			[If, ((FRAC(#slKeepGrpFlags / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups"], [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slFreezeBorderFlags / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder], [IPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slFreezeGroupsFlags / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups], [IPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slGeoHalf / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Half], [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Half]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slGeoDouble / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Double], [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Double]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slGeoSame / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Same], [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Same]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slGeoAdapt / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt], [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slUsePPFlags / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"], [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize, #slAdaptSizePrior]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", #slCurveStr]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups", #slSmoothSet]
			
			// Get the flags of the Divide
			[VarSet, slSmt, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Smt]]
			[VarSet, slSuv, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Suv]]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSmt / 2)) == 0), [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Smt]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSuv / 2)) > 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Suv]]
			
			[Note, "Step 2 - Please wait... Subdividing polymesh..."]
			[Delay,1]
			[If, #slAdaptRes ,
				[Loop, #slSubDivCount,
					[MergeUndo]
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Divide]
				]
				
			,
				[VarSet,slLoopCtr,[IGet, "ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl"]]
				
				[Loop, (#slLoopCtr-1),
					[MergeUndo]
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Divide]
				]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSmt / 2)) == 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Smt], [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Smt]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSuv / 2)) > 0), [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Suv], [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Suv]]
			[IPress,Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[If, #slHasSDynamic ,
				[IUnPress,  "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]
				[MergeUndo]
			]
			[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			// Create Morph Target for later use
			[VarSet, slDelMTStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
			[If, #slDelMTStat ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress,"Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
				
			]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress,"Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]  // New undo point
				
			[ToolSelect, #slToolLast]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			
			[IPress, "Edit:Tool:DelUH"]
			[MergeUndo]
			
			[ISet,Tool:Geometry:SDiv, 1]
			// Get the flags of these buttons
			[VarSet, slFarthest, [IGetFlags, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]]
			[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Inner]
			[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Outer]
			//Change the setting of the ProjectAll
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, .025]
			[ISet, "Tool:SubTool:PA Blur", 2]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Mean, 25]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Dist, .25]
			[IModSet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell,7]
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Inner]
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]
// 			[If, (#slFarthest == 9) || (#slFarthest == 11) || (#slFarthest == 13) || (#slFarthest == 1),
// 				[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]
// 			,]
			
			[Note, "Step 3 - Please wait... Projection Passes..."]
			
			[IUnPress, Transform:Solo]

			[MergeUndo]
			[IKeyPress,'1',
				[IPress, Tool:SubTool:ProjectAll]
			]
			
			[IPress, Transform:Solo]

			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv,[IGetMax, Tool:Geometry:SDiv]]
			//Get the area of the bounding box
			[VArSet, slBoxWidthAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxHeightAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxLengthAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaAfter, (#slBoxWidthAfter * #slBoxHeightAfter * #slBoxLengthAfter)]
			[VarSet,slBoxAreaDiff, (#slBoxAreaAfter - #slBoxAreaBefore) ]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaPerc, (#slBoxAreaDiff / #slBoxAreaBefore )]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaPerc, (#slBoxAreaPerc * #slBoxAreaBefore)]
			[VarSet,slBoxProject, 0]
			[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Inner]
			[If, (#slBoxAreaAfter - #slBoxAreaBefore) < 0 ,
				[VarSet,slBoxProject, ((#slBoxAreaPerc*2)*-1)]
			,
				[VarSet,slBoxProject, #slBoxAreaPerc]
			]
			
			//[ISet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, (#slBoxProject * 4)]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, .025]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Mean, 25]
			[VarSet, slTheDist, .25]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Dist,#slTheDist ]
			
			[IUnPress, Transform:Solo]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IKeyPress,'1',
				[IPress, Tool:SubTool:ProjectAll]
			]
			
			[IPress, Transform:Solo]
			
			[SubtoolSelect,0]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy] 
			[VarSet, slLastToolVisited,[ToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[ToolSelect, #slToolIndex]
			[VarSet, slActiveTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[SubToolSelect, (#slActiveTool - 1)]
			[VarSet, slResGeo, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
			[VarSet, slPolishGeo, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
			[If, (#slResGeo == 995) &&  (#slPolishGeo == 64) ,
				[IKeyPress,'2',
					[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Delete]
				]
				[VarSet, slActiveTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			,
				[SubToolSelect, #slActiveTool]
			]
			
			
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Duplicate]
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:MoveUp]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[ISet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size", .5]
			
			[IPress, "Tool:Polypaint:Colorize"]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetRGB, 255, 0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetMaterial, "MatCap Red Wax"]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IPress,"Edit:Tool:DelUH"]
			
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:SelectDown]
			[ToolSelect, #slLastToolVisited]
			[SubtoolSelect,1]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy] // Copy the final projected mesh.
			[IKeyPress, '2',
				[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Del All"]
			]
			
			[ToolSelect, #slToolIndex]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace] // PAste the projeted details on the morph side 2
			[If, #slSymmetryOn,
				[IPress, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]
			]
			[SubToolSelect, #slActiveTool]
			[VarSet, slIndex, -1]
			[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slIndex]
			[VarSet, slSuccess, -1]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideSubTool,#slIndex, slSuccess]
			[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slIndex]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Res", 995]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish", 64]
			[SubToolSelect, (#slActiveTool+1)]
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
		
		]// END LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1,
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh] // Need to doublecehck other script for the restore placement
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[If, #slErrorToDisplay == 0 ,
				[If, ((FRAC(#slMrgbFlag / 2)) > 0), [IPress,Draw:Mrgb], [IUnPress,Draw:Mrgb]]
				[If, ((FRAC(#slRgbFlag / 2)) > 0), [IPress,Draw:Rgb], [IUnPress,Draw:Rgb]]
				[If, ((FRAC(#slMFlag / 2)) > 0), [IPress,Draw:M], [IUnPress,Draw:M]]
			]
			
		]	
		
	]//END FREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError,
		[VarSet, slErrorStrLength, [StrLength, #slErrorToDisplay]]
		[If, #slErrorStrLength > 0,
			[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
		]
	]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
	[If, #slErrorToDisplay == 0 ,
		[IFreeze,
			[SubToolSelect, ([SubtoolGetActiveIndex] - 1)]
			[VarSet, slSubToolName, ""]
			[RoutineCall,J_SubToolNameGet, slSubToolName]
			[VarSet,slNewName,[StrMerge, "FPTM||", #slSubToolName]]
			[VarSet,slButtonPath,"Tool:SubTool:Rename"]
			[FileExecute, [Var, gFileUtilsPath], RenameSetNext, slNewName]
			[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Rename"]
			[SubToolSelect, ([SubtoolGetActiveIndex] + 1)]
		]
	]
	
	

]

[IButton, ReMeshq, "",
	[IkeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_RemeshQ]
	]
	
]
[Loop,1,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemSDivNoteToggle",1,0]
	[If, [MVarGet, jmemSDivNoteToggle,0] < 0,
		[MVarGet, jmemSDivNoteToggle,0,0]
	,]
]

[RoutineDef, J_ZMeshq,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[VarSet, slMrgbFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:Mrgb]]
	[VarSet, slRgbFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:Rgb]]
	[VarSet, slMFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:M]]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
				
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay,1, 1, 1, 1, 1]
			//...continue
			
			[NOTE, "Please wait..."]
			[VarSet, slAltPressed, 0]
			[VarSet, slAltFlags, [IgetFlags, "ZScript:AltRemeshing"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slAltFlags/2)) > 0), [VarSet, slAltPressed, 1]]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slCurveDelStat, [IGetStatus, "Stroke:Delete"]]
			[VarSet, slAdaptResFlags,[IGetFlags,"ZScript:AdaptRes"]]
			[VarSet, slAdaptRes, 0]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slAdaptResFlags/2)) > 0), [VarSet, slAdaptRes, 1]]
			
			[IPress,Draw:Mrgb]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[VarSet, slToolIndex, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]	//Save first the index of the active ztool
			//Check if thereis subdiv then bake
			[VArSet, slDynamicFlags,[IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]]
			[VArSet,slHasSDynamic, 0]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slDynamicFlags/2)) > 0), [VArSet,slHasSDynamic, 1]]
			
			[If, #slHasSDynamic ,
				[If, [MVarGet, jmemSDivNoteToggle,0] == 1 ,
					[Ipress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Apply"]
					[MergeUndo]
					[VarSet,slDelLowerStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
					[If, #slDelLowerStat ,
						 [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]
					]
				,
					[VarSet,slFlatDiv,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:s.FlatSubdiv"]]
					[VarSet,slSmtDiv,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:s.SmoothSubdiv"]]
					[VarSet, slDivCnt, #slFlatDiv]
					[If, #slSmtDiv > #slFlatDiv ,
						[VarSet, slDivCnt, #slSmtDiv]
					]
					// Get the points of the mesh
					[VarSet, slMeshInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
					[VarSet, slPointsId, [StrFind, "Points=", #slMeshInfo]]
					[VarSet, slHiddenPolys,[StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slMeshInfo]]
					[VarSet, slOrigPoints, [StrExtract, slMeshInfo, (#slPointsId + 7), (#slHiddenPolys - 2)]]
					[VarSet,slPointsLog,(LOG10(#slOrigPoints * .001))]
					[VArSet, slLogConst,(.60205999132798 * #slDivCnt )]
					[VarSet, slSubDivPoints,(10^^(#slPointsLog + #slLogConst))]
					
					[VArSet,slTheRoundedPoints, 0]
					[VarSet,slTheMessage, ""]
					[If, #slSubDivPoints >= 1000 ,
						[VArSet,slTheRoundedPoints, (#slSubDivPoints * .001)]
						[If, #slSubDivPoints <= 3000 ,
							[VarSet,slTheMessage,[StrMerge,"The output polys will be around ", #slTheRoundedPoints, " million because Dynamic Subdivision is on. Would you like it to be turned off before proceeding?"]]
							[NoteIButton, "OK"]
							[NoteIButton, "NO"]
							[NoteIButton, "Always OK(skip this note until bext restart)"]
						,
							[VarSet,slTheMessage,"The output polys will be more than 3 million because Dynamic Subdivision is on. Action is going to be canceled."]
							[NoteIButton, "OK"]
						]
						
					,
						[VArSet,slTheRoundedPoints, (#slSubDivPoints * 1000)]
						[VarSet,slTheMessage,[StrMerge,"The output polys will be around ", #slTheRoundedPoints, " because Dynamic Subdivision is on. Would you like it to be turned off before proceeding?"]]
						[NoteIButton, "OK"]
						[NoteIButton, "NO"]
						[NoteIButton, "Always OK(skip this note until bext restart)"]
					]
					
					
					[VarSet, slDynamicNote,[NOte, #slTheMessage]]
					[If, #slDynamicNote == 1 ,
						[If, #slSubDivPoints > 3000,
							[VaRSet, slHasError, 1]
							[LoopExit]
						,
							[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]
						]
						
					]
					[If,  #slDynamicNote == 2 ,
						[Ipress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Apply"]
						[MergeUndo]
						[VarSet,slDelLowerStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
						[If, #slDelLowerStat ,
							 [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]
						]
					]
					[If,  #slDynamicNote == 3 ,
						[MVarSet, jmemSDivNoteToggle,0, 1]
						[Ipress, "Tool:Geometry:s.Apply"]
						[MergeUndo]
						[VarSet,slDelLowerStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
						[If, #slDelLowerStat ,
							 [IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]
						]
					]
				
				]
				
				
			,]
			[IPress, Tool:Clone]
			[VarSet, slToolLast, ([ToolGetCount] - 1)]
			
			//Get the area of the bounding box
			[VArSet, slBoxWidthBefore, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxHeightBefore, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxLengthBefore, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaBefore, (#slBoxWidthBefore * #slBoxHeightBefore * #slBoxLengthBefore)]
			//Check for any symmetry
			
			[VarSet, slSymmetryOn, 0]
			[VarSet, slXMirFlags, [IGetFlags, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slXMirFlags/2)) > 0),
				[VarSet, slSymmetryOn, 1]
			]
			// Get the flags of these buttons
			[VarSet, slUsePP,[IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]]
			[VarSet, slFreezeBorder, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder]]
			[VarSet, slFreezeGroups, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups]]
			[VarSet, slKeepGroups, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups]]
			[VarSet, slTgtPolyCount, [IGet, Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count]]
			[VarSet, slAdapt, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]]
			[VarSet, slHalf, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Half]]
			[VarSet, slSame, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Same]]
			[VarSet, slDouble, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Double]]
			[VarSet, slAdaptSize, [IGet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize]]
			[VarSet, slCurveStrength, [IGet, Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength]]
			[VarSet, slSmoothGrps, [IGet, Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups]]
			// Now set the settings
			[If, ((FRAC(#slFreezeBorder/2)) > 0),
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slFreezeGroups/2)) > 0),[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slUsePP/2)) > 0),[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]]
			[VarSet, slHasPolyGorups, 0]
			[VarSet, slHasCurves, 0]
			[If, #slCurveDelStat,[VarSet, slHasCurves, 1]]
			[VarSet, slMaskingBorder, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingBorder/2)) > 0),[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			[VarSet, slMaskingCrease, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingCrease/2)) > 0),[IUnPress,  "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[VarSet, slMaskingGroups, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingGroups/2)) == 0),[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			[VarSet, slUndoStep, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[IPress,"Tool:Masking:MaskByFeature"]
			[VarSet, slUndoStepAfter, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoStepAfter >  #slUndoStep,
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
				[VarSet, slHasPolyGorups, 1]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingBorder/2)) > 0),[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingCrease/2)) > 0),[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMaskingGroups/2)) == 0),[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			[If, #slHasPolyGorups,
				[If, ((FRAC(#slKeepGroups/2)) == 0),
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups]
				]
			,
				[If, ((FRAC(#slKeepGroups/2)) > 0),
					[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups]
				]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slAdapt/2)) > 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slHalf/2)) > 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Half]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSame/2)) > 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Same]]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slDouble/2)) > 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Double]]
			
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize, 50]
			[If, #slHasCurves,
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 100]
			,
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 0]
			]
			[ISet,Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups, 1]
			//..Continue
			
			// Get the points of the mesh
			[VarSet, slMeshInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
			[VarSet, slPointsId, [StrFind, "Points=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolys,[StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slOrigPoints, [StrExtract, slMeshInfo, (#slPointsId + 7), (#slHiddenPolys - 2)]]
			[If, #slAdaptRes == 0 ,
				[VarSet, slTargetHighest,[IGet, "ZScript:Highest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]]
				[VarSet, slOrigPoints, (#slTargetHighest * 1000)]
			]
			[VarSet, slLogConstant, .60205999132798]
			[VarSet, slOrigPointsLog, (LOG10(#slOrigPoints * .001))]
			[VarSet,slSubDivCount,((INT(#slOrigPointsLog / #slLogConstant)) - 1)]
			[VarSet, slConstantMult,(#slLogConstant * #slSubDivCount)]
			[VarSEt, slTheBase, (10 ^^ (#slOrigPointsLog- #slConstantMult))]
			[VarSet, slTargetCount, 0]
			[If, #slAdaptRes ,
				[If, #slSymmetryOn ,
					[If, #slAltPressed ,
						[VarSet, slTargetCount, (#slTheBase)]
					,
						[VarSet, slTargetCount, (#slTheBase*.5)]
					]
				,
					[VarSet, slTargetCount, #slTheBase]
				]
			,
				[VarSet, slTargetLowest,[IGet, "ZScript:Lowest Lvl Polys 1=1k"]]
				[VarSet, slTargetCount, #slTargetLowest]
				
				[If, #slSymmetryOn ,
					[If, !#slAltPressed ,
						[VarSet, slTargetCount, (#slTargetCount*.5)]
					]
				]
			]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", #slTargetCount]
			
			[If, #slAltPressed ,
				[Note, "Step 1 - Please wait... Now remeshing (ALT)..."]
			,
				[Note, "Step 1 - Please wait... Now remeshing..."]
			]
			
			
			[If, #slAltPressed ,
				[IKeyPress, ALT,
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
				]
			,
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
			]

			// Get the points of the mesh and remesh until it gets the right target
			[VarSet, slMeshInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
			[VarSet, slPointsId, [StrFind, "Points=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolys,[StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slPoints, [StrExtract, #slMeshInfo, (#slPointsId + 7), (#slHiddenPolys - 2)]]
			[VarSet, slRemeshAgain, 0]
			[VArSet, slPointsLog, (LOG10(#slPoints * .001))]
			[VarSet, slConstantMult,(#slLogConstant * #slSubDivCount)]
			[VarSet,slHighestSubDLog, (#slPointsLog + #slConstantMult)]
			[VarSet,slFinalSubDCount, ((10 ^^ #slHighestSubDLog) * 1000)]
			[VarSet, slPointsDiff, (ABS(#slFinalSubDCount - #slOrigPoints ))]
			[VarSet, slLimit,3000]
			[If, #slPointsDiff >= #slLimit ,
				[VarSet, slRemeshAgain, 1]
			]

			[If,#slRemeshAgain,
				
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[If, #slHasSDynamic ,
					[IUnPress,  "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]
					[MergeUndo]
				]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
				[VarSet, slOrigLog,(LOG10(#slOrigPoints * .001))]
				[VarSet, slOrigSubD,(LOG10(#slFinalSubDCount * .001))]
				[VarSet, slLeftSide, ((#slOrigSubD-#slOrigLog)/ #slOrigLog)]
				[VarSet, slZRemRes, (1.05*(#slTargetCount + #slLeftSide))]
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", #slZRemRes]
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]
				[ISet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize, 50]
				[If, #slHasCurves,
					
					[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 100]
				,
					[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 0]
				]
				
				[If, #slAltPressed,
					[IKeyPress, ALT,
						[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
					]
				
				,
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher]
				]
				
			]
			
			// Reset the settings
			[If, ((FRAC(#slFreezeBorder/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder]
			,
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slFreezeGroups/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups]
			,
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slUsePP/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]
			,
				[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slKeepGroups/2)) == 0),
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups]
			,
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slAdapt/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]
			,
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Adapt]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slHalf/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Half]
			,
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Half]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSame/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Same]
			,
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Same]
			]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slDouble/2)) > 0),
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Double]
			,
				[IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Double]
			]
			
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize, #slAdaptSize]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", #slCurveStrength]
			[ISet,Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups, #slSmoothGrps]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", #slTgtPolyCount]
			
			// Get the points of the mesh
			[VarSet, slMeshInfo, [IGetInfo, "Tool:Current Tool"]]
			[VarSet, slPointsId, [StrFind, "Points=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPolys,[StrFind, "HiddenPolys=", #slMeshInfo]]
			[VarSet, slPoints, [StrExtract, #slMeshInfo, (#slPointsId + 7), (#slHiddenPolys - 2)]]
			[VarSet, slOldPoly, #slPoints]
			
			// Get the flags of the Divide
			[VarSet, slSmt, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Smt]]
			[VarSet, slSuv, [IGetFlags, Tool:Geometry:Suv]]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSmt/2)) == 0), [IPress, Tool:Geometry:Smt]]
			
			[If, ((FRAC(#slSuv/2)) > 0), [IUnPress, Tool:Geometry:Suv]]
			
			[Note, "Step 2 - Please wait... Subdividing polymesh..."]
			[Delay,1]
			
			[If, #slAdaptRes ,
				[Loop, #slSubDivCount,
					[MergeUndo]
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Divide]
				]
				
			,
				[VarSet,slLoopCtr,[IGet, "ZScript:Highest SubD Lvl"]]
				
				[Loop, (#slLoopCtr-1),
					[MergeUndo]
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Divide]
				]
			
			]
			
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Copy"]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[If, #slHasSDynamic ,
				[IUnPress,  "Tool:Geometry:s.Dynamic"]
				[MergeUndo]
			]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[VarSet,slMorphStat,[IGetStatus,"Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
			[If, #slMorphStat ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress,"Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
			]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress,"Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
			
			//..Continue
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[ToolSelect, #slToolLast]
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Duplicate]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[IPress, "Edit:Tool:DelUH"]
			[If, #slHasPolyGorups ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[ISet,"Tool:Geometry:SDiv", [IGet,"Tool:Geometry:SDiv"]]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Copy"]
				[VarSet, slPGCov,[IGEt, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage"]]
				[VarSet, slPGClstr,[IGEt, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr"]]
				[ISEt, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage", 1]
				[ISEt, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr", 1]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress,"Tool:Polygroups:GroupVisible"]
				[ISEt, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage", #slPGCov]
				[ISEt, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr", #slPGClstr]
				
			]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet,Tool:Geometry:SDiv, 1]
			// Get the flags of these buttons
			[VarSet, slFarthest, [IGetFlags, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]]
			[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Inner]
			[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Outer]
			//Change the setting of the ProjectAll
			//Get the area of the bounding box
			[VArSet, slBoxWidthAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxHeightAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxLengthAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaAfter, (#slBoxWidthAfter * #slBoxHeightAfter * #slBoxLengthAfter)]
			[VarSet,slBoxAreaDiff,(#slBoxAreaAfter - #slBoxAreaBefore) ]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaPerc, (#slBoxAreaDiff / #slBoxAreaBefore )]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaPerc, (#slBoxAreaPerc * #slBoxAreaBefore)]
			[VarSet,slBoxProject, ((#slBoxAreaPerc*2)*-1)]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, .025]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Mean,25]
			[ISet, "Tool:SubTool:PA Blur", 2]
			
			[VarSet, slTheDist, .25]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Dist, #slTheDist]
			[IModSet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, 7]
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]
// 			[If, (#slFarthest == 9) || (#slFarthest == 11) || (#slFarthest == 13) || (#slFarthest == 1),
// 				[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]
// 			,]
			
			[Note, "Step 3 - Please wait... Projection Passes..."]
			
			[IUnPress, Transform:Solo]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IKeyPress,'1',
				[IPress, Tool:SubTool:ProjectAll]
			]
			
			[IPress, Transform:Solo]
			//Get the area of the bounding box
			[VArSet, slBoxWidthAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:X Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxHeightAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Y Size"]]
			[VArSet, slBoxLengthAfter, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Z Size"]]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaAfter, (#slBoxWidthAfter * #slBoxHeightAfter * #slBoxLengthAfter)]
			[VarSet,slBoxAreaDiff,(#slBoxAreaAfter - #slBoxAreaBefore) ]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaPerc, (#slBoxAreaDiff / #slBoxAreaBefore )]
			[VarSet, slBoxAreaPerc, (#slBoxAreaPerc * #slBoxAreaBefore)]
			[VarSet,slBoxProject, ((#slBoxAreaPerc*2)*-1)]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, .025]
			[VarSet, slTheDist, .25]
			[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Dist, #slTheDist]
			
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv,[IGetMax, Tool:Geometry:SDiv]]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Solo]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IKeyPress,'1',
				[IPress, Tool:SubTool:ProjectAll]
			]
			
			[IPress, Transform:Solo]
			[If, #slHasPolyGorups ,
				[VArSet, slDelMTStat,[IGeTStatus,"Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
				[If, #slDelMTStat ,
					[MergeUndo]
					[IPress,"Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
				]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress,"Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress,"Tool:Morph Target:Switch"]
			]
			
			[SubToolSelect,0]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy] 
			[VarSet, slLastVisitedTool,[ToolGetActiveIndex]]
						
			[ToolSelect, #slToolIndex]
			[VarSet, slActiveTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[SubToolSelect, (#slActiveTool - 1)]
			[VarSet, slResGeo, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
			[VarSet, slPolishGeo, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
			[If, (#slResGeo == 995) &&  (#slPolishGeo == 64) ,
				[IKeyPress,'2',
					[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Delete]
				]
				[VarSet, slActiveTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			,
				[SubToolSelect, #slActiveTool]
			]
			
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Duplicate]
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:MoveUp]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[VarSet, slMrgbFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:Mrgb]]
			[VarSet, slRgbFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:Rgb]]
			[VarSet, slMFlag, [IGetFlags,Draw:M]]
			
			[IPress, "Tool:Polypaint:Colorize"]

				[IPress,Draw:Mrgb]
			,
				[IUnPress,Draw:Mrgb]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slRgbFlag/2)) > 0),
				[IPress,Draw:Rgb]
			,
				[IUnPress,Draw:Rgb]
			]
			[If, ((FRAC(#slMFlag/2)) > 0),
				[IPress,Draw:M]
			,
				[IUnPress,Draw:M]
			]
			[ISet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size", .5]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IPress, "Edit:Tool:DelUH"]

			[SubToolSelect, 1]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy] // Copy the final projected mesh.
			[IKeyPress, '2',
				[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Del All"]
			]

			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:SelectDown]

			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace] // PAste the projeted details on the morph side 2
			[If, #slSymmetryOn,
				[IPress, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]
			]
			[SubToolSelect, #slActiveTool]
			[VarSet, slIndex, -1]
			[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slIndex]
			[VarSet, slSuccess, -1]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideSubTool,#slIndex, slSuccess]
			[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slIndex]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Res", 995]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish", 64]
			[SubToolSelect, (#slActiveTool+1)]
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
			
		
		]// END LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1,
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh] // Need to doublecehck other script for the restore placement
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[If,  #slHasError == 0,
				[If, ((FRAC(#slMrgbFlag/2)) > 0),
					[IPress,Draw:Mrgb]
				,
					[IUnPress,Draw:Mrgb]
				]
				[If, ((FRAC(#slRgbFlag/2)) > 0),
					[IPress,Draw:Rgb]
				,
					[IUnPress,Draw:Rgb]
				]
				[If, ((FRAC(#slMFlag/2)) > 0),
					[IPress,Draw:M]
				,
					[IUnPress,Draw:M]
				]
			]
		]	
		
	]//END FREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError,	
		[VArSet,slErrorLength,[StrLength, #slErrorToDisplay]]
		[If, #slErrorLength > 0,
			[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
		]
		
	]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
	[If,  !#slHasError,
		[IFreeze,
			[SubToolSelect, ([SubtoolGetActiveIndex] - 1)]
			[VarSet, slSubToolName, ""]
			[RoutineCall,J_SubToolNameGet, slSubToolName]
			[VarSet,slNewName,[StrMerge, "FPTM||", #slSubToolName]]

			[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Rename"]

		]

[RoutineDef, J_FixProjq,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay , ""]
	[VArSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[VarSet, slIndex0Error, "The index of the active subtool is 0. Action cancelled."]
	[VarSet, slIndexBeforeError,  "The subtool before the active one is invalid. Action cancelled."]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			
			[VarSet, slActiveSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[If, #slActiveSubTool < 1,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]

			]

			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]

			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]

			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9122, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]

			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9125, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]

			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9129, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			
			[SubToolSelect, (#slActiveSubTool-1)]

			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9132, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slIndex]
			[VarSet, slSuccess, -1]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9135, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ShowSubTool, #slIndex, slSuccess]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9137, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size", .5]
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetRGB, 255, 0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9141, 0, 0, 0, 0]

			[IPress, Edit:Tool:DelUH]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9145, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[SubToolSelect, #slActiveSubTool]
			
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9149, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Solo]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9151, 0, 0, 0, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			
		]// END OF LOOP
		
	]// END OF FREEZE
	[If, #slHasError == 1,
		[NOte, #slErrorToDisplay]
	
	,]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]

[Loop, 1,

[RoutineDef, J_IsolateProjQ,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			
			
			
			[Note, "Isolating. Please wait..."]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			
			[VarSet, slSelSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			
			[VArSet, slValid, 0]
			[VArSet, slSelection, 0]
			//Check if it is the clone
			[VArSet, slSubToolCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
			[If,  #slSubToolCount < 2,
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			[VArSet, slFixID, [MVarGet, jmemIsoFixID, 0]]
				
			[If, #slSubToolCount == 2 ,
				[VarSet, slToolID, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[VArSet, slFixID, [MVarGet, jmemIsoFixID, 0]]
				[If, #slToolID ==  #slFixID,
					
					[SubToolSelect, 1]
					[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
					[IKeyPress, '2',
						[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Del All"]
					]
					
					[VarSet, slOrigTool,[MVarGet,jmemIsoFixOrigID, 0 ]]
					[ToolSelect, #slOrigTool]
					[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
					[MVarSet,jmemIsoFixOrigID, 0, -1 ]
					[MVarSet,jmemIsoFixID, 0, -1 ]
					[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
				
				//...continue
				[VarSet, slSelSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[SubToolSelect, 0]
				[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
				[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
				[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
					[VarSet, slIsVisible, 0]
					[VarSet, slPolDisp, [IGet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size"]]
					[If, #slPolDisp == .5 ,
						[VArSet, slTheID, 0]
						[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slTheID]
						[VarSet, slIsVisible, -1]
						[RoutineCall, J_SubToolVisibility, #slTheID, slIsVisible]
						[If, #slSelSubTool == 1 ,
							[SubToolSelect, 1]
						,]
						[If, !#slIsVisible ,
							[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
							[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
							[LoopExit]
						,]
					,
						[If, #slSelSubTool == 1 ,
							[SubToolSelect, 1]
						,]
						[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
						[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
						[LoopExit]
					]
						
					
				,]
				[If, #slSelSubTool == 1 ,
					[SubToolSelect, 1]
				,]
				[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
					
					
					[VarSet, slValid, 1]
					[VArSet, slSelection, #slSelSubTool]
				,
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Action can not proceed. Mesh invalid for isolation. You must ZMeshq this first."]
					[LoopExit]
				]
			,]
			[If, #slSubToolCount > 2 ,
				[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
				[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
				[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
					[VarSet, slIsVisible, 0]
					[VarSet, slPolDisp, [IGet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size"]]
					[If, #slPolDisp == .5 ,
						[VArSet, slTheID, 0]
						[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slTheID]
						[VarSet, slIsVisible, -1]
						[RoutineCall, J_SubToolVisibility, #slTheID, slIsVisible]
						[If, !#slIsVisible ,
							[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
							[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
							[LoopExit]
						,]
					,
						[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
						[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
						[LoopExit]
					]
						
					[VarSet, slValid, 1]
					[VArSet, slSelection, 0]
				,
					[SubToolSelect, (#slSelSubTool - 1)]
					[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
					[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
					
					[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
						[VarSet, slIsVisible, 0]
						[VarSet, slPolDisp, [IGet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size"]]
						
						[If, #slPolDisp == .5 ,
							[VArSet, slTheID, 0]
							[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slTheID]
							[VarSet, slIsVisible, -1]
							[RoutineCall, J_SubToolVisibility, #slTheID, slIsVisible]
							[SubToolSelect, #slSelSubTool]
							[If, !#slIsVisible ,
								[SubToolSelect, #slSelSubTool]
								[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
								[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
								[LoopExit]
							,]
						,	
							[SubToolSelect, #slSelSubTool]
							[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
							[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
							[LoopExit]
						]
						[SubToolSelect, #slSelSubTool]
						[VarSet, slValid, 1]
						[VArSet, slSelection, 1]
					,
						[SubToolSelect, #slSelSubTool]
						[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
						[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
						[LoopExit]
					]
				]
			,]
			
			[If, #slValid ,
				[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 9331,9357]
				[VarSet, slSelectedTool, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9333, 0,0,0,0]
				[MVarSet, jmemIsoFixOrigID, 0, #slSelectedTool]
				[If, #slSelection == 1 ,
					[SubToolSelect, ([SubToolGetActiveIndex]-1)]
				,]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9338, 0,0,0,0]
				[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Clone]
				[SubToolSelect, ([SubToolGetActiveIndex]+1)]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9341, 0,0,0,0]
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:Switch"]
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:Switch"]
				[VarSet, slColorizeFlags, [IGetFlags, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
				[ToolSelect, ([ToolGetCount]-1)]
				[VarSet, slFixID, ([ToolGetCount]-1)]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9345, 0,0,0,0]
				[MVarSet, jmemIsoFixID, 0, #slFixID]
				[VaRSet, slTemp, [MVarGet, jmemIsoFixID, 0]]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9348, 0,0,0,0]
				[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Insert"]
				[IPress, "PopUp:PolyMesh3D"]
				
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9350, 0,0,0,0]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace]
				[VarSet, slMorphStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
				[If, #slMorphStat ,
					[MergeUndo]
					[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
					
				,]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
				[ToolSelect, #slSelectedTool]
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
				[ToolSelect, ([ToolGetCount]-1)]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:Switch"]
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
				
				[VarSet, slPolyDrawSize, [IGet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size"]]
				[If, #slPolyDrawSize < 1,
					[MergeUndo]
				,]
				[ISet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size", 1]
				[If, (#slColorizeFlags == 9) || (#slColorizeFlags == 11) || (#slColorizeFlags == 13) || (#slColorizeFlags == 1),
					[VarSet, slCurrentColorize, [IGetFLags, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
					[If, (#slCurrentColorize == 8) || (#slCurrentColorize == 10) || (#slCurrentColorize == 0)|| (#slCurrentColorize == 10),
						[MergeUndo]
						[IPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
					,]
				,]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9354, 0,0,0,0]
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:DelUH]
				[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 9357, 0,0,0,0]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			,]
			
		]// END OF MAIN LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
			
		,]
	
	]// END OF IFREEZE

		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]

[RoutineDef, J_ProjQ,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[VarSet,slProjectMessage, "Projecting the details. Please wait."]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1]
			//..continue
			[Note, "Projecting. Please wait..."]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			
			[VarSet, slSelSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			
			[VArSet, slValid, 0]
			[VArSet, slSelection, 0]
			//Check if it is the clone
			[VArSet, slSubToolCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
			[If,  #slSubToolCount < 2,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The ZTool has only 1 subtool.Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			
			//..continue
			[VArSet, slFixID, [MVarGet, jmemIsoFixID, 0]]
				
			[If, #slSubToolCount == 2 ,
				[VarSet, slToolID, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[VArSet, slFixID, [MVarGet, jmemIsoFixID, 0]]
				[If, #slToolID ==  #slFixID,
					[SubToolSelect, 0]
					[VarSet, slColorizeFlags0,[IGetFlags, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
					[If, (#slColorizeFlags0 == 9) || (#slColorizeFlags0 == 11)  || (#slColorizeFlags0 == 13)  || (#slColorizeFlags0 == 1) ,
						[IUnPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
					,]
					[SubToolSelect, 1]
					[VarSet, slColorizeFlags1,[IGetFlags, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]]
					[If, (#slColorizeFlags1 == 9) || (#slColorizeFlags1 == 11)  || (#slColorizeFlags1 == 13)  || (#slColorizeFlags1 == 1) ,
						[IUnPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
					,]
					[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Mean, 25]
					[ISet, "Tool:SubTool:PA Blur", 2]
					[IModSet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, 7]
					[VarSet, slFarthest, [IGetFlags, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]]
					[VarSet, slInnerFlags, [IGetFlags, Tool:SubTool:Inner]]
					[VarSet, slOuterFlags, [IGetFlags, Tool:SubTool:Outer]]
					[If, (#slFarthest == 9) || (#slFarthest == 11) || (#slFarthest == 13) || (#slFarthest == 1),
						[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Farthest]
					,]
					
					[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Outer]
					[IUnPress, Tool:SubTool:Inner]
					[ISet, Tool:SubTool:Dist, .025]
					[ISet, Tool:SubTool:ProjectionShell, .001]
					[IShow, 1011]
					[Note, #slProjectMessage]
					[IKeyPress,'2',
						[IPress, Tool:SubTool:ProjectAll]
					]
					[IHide, 1011]
					
					[SubToolSelect, 0]
					
					[If, (#slColorizeFlags0 == 9) || (#slColorizeFlags0 == 11)  || (#slColorizeFlags0 == 13)  || (#slColorizeFlags0 == 1) ,
						[IPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
					,]
					[SubToolSelect, 1]
					[If, (#slColorizeFlags1 == 9) || (#slColorizeFlags1 == 11)  || (#slColorizeFlags1 == 13)  || (#slColorizeFlags1 == 1) ,
						[IPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
					,]
					[If, (#slInnerFlags == 9) || (#slInnerFlags == 11)  || (#slInnerFlags == 13)  || (#slInnerFlags == 1) ,
						[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Inner]
					,]
					[If, (#slOuterFlags == 9) || (#slOuterFlags == 11)  || (#slOuterFlags == 13)  || (#slOuterFlags == 1) ,
						[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Outer]
					,]
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Projection successful."]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
				
				//..continue...
				
				[VarSet, slSelSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[SubToolSelect, 0]
				[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
				[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
				
				[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
					
					[VarSet, slIsVisible, 0]
					[VarSet, slPolDisp, [IGet, "Tool:Display Properties:Polygons Draw Size"]]
					[If, #slPolDisp == .5 ,
						[VArSet, slTheID, 0]
						[RoutineCall, J_SubToolListGetSelected2, slTheID]
						[VarSet, slIsVisible, -1]
						[RoutineCall, J_SubToolVisibility, #slTheID, slIsVisible]
						
						[If, !#slIsVisible ,
							[SubToolSelect, #slSelSubTool]
							[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
							[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "You must click FixProjq first.Action cancelled."]
							[LoopExit]
						,	
						
							[IPress, "Tool:Clone"]
							[SubToolSelect, 1]
							[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
							[ToolSelect, ([ToolGetCount] - 1)]
							[MergeUndo]
							[IUnPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
							[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Insert]
							[IPress, PopUp:PolyMesh3D]
							[MergeUndo]
							[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
							[MergeUndo]
							[IUnPress, Tool:Polypaint:Colorize]
							[IPress, Edit:Tool:DelUH]

[RoutineDef, J_DelZMeshQ,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			
			[Note, "Deleting. Please wait..."]
			[VarSet, slScrollBarPos, [IGetSecondary, Tool:SubTool:Subtool Scrollbar]]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			
			[VarSet, slSelSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			
			//Check if it is the clone
			[VArSet, slSubToolCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
			[If,  #slSubToolCount < 2,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The ZTool has only 1 subtool.Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
				
			[If, #slSubToolCount == 2 ,
				[SubToolSelect, 0]
				[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
				[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
				[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
					[IKeyPress, '2', 
						[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Delete"]
					]
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "1 subtool has been deleted."]
					[LoopExit]
					
				,
					[If, #slSelSubTool == 1 ,
						[SubToolSelect, 1]
					,]
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay,  "Nothing has been deleted."]
					
					[LoopExit]
				]
				
				
			,]
			[If, #slSubToolCount > 2 ,
				[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
				[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
				[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
					[IKeyPress, '2', 
						[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Delete"]
					]
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "1 subtool has been deleted."]
					[LoopExit]
				,
					[SubToolSelect, (#slSelSubTool - 1)]
					[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
					[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
					
					[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
						[IKeyPRess, '2', 
							[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Delete"]
						]
						[SubToolSelect, (#slSelSubTool - 1)]
						[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
						[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "1 subtool has been deleted."]
						[LoopExit]
					,
						[SubToolSelect, #slSelSubTool]
						[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
						[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay,  "Nothing has been deleted."]
					
						[LoopExit]
					]
				]
			,]
			
			
		]// END OF MAIN LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
			
		,]
	
	]// END OF IFREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]

[IButton, DelProjq, "",
	[IKeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_DelZMeshQ]
	]
	
]



[RoutineDef, J_CleanZMeshQ,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			
			[Note, "Cleaning. Please wait..."]
			[VarSet, slScrollBarPos, [IGetSecondary, Tool:SubTool:Subtool Scrollbar]]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_FixGhostingCrash]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 6260, 6306]
			//Check if it is the clone
			[VArSet, slSubToolCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
			[If,  #slSubToolCount < 2,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The ZTool has only 1 subtool.Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 6268, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slSelSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[VarSet, slCtr, 0]
			[VArSet, slID, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 6272, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slDelCount, 0]
			[Loop, #slSubToolCount,
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 6268, #slSubToolCount, #slCtr,6274,6288]
				
				[IUpdate, 1]
				[SubToolSelect, #slID]
				
				[VarSet, slShadRes,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Res"]]
				[VarSet, slShadPolish,[IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polish"]]
				[If, (#slShadRes == 995) && (#slShadPolish == 64) ,
					[IKeyPress, '2', 
						[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Delete"]
					]
					[IUpdate, 1]
					[VarDec, slSelSubTool]
					[VarInc, slDelCount]
				,
				
					[VarInc, slID]
				]
				[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 6287, #slSubToolCount, #slCtr,6274,6289]
				[VarInc, slCtr]
				[If, #slCtr ==  #slSubToolCount,
					[IUpdate, 1]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
				
			]// END OF LOOP
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 6295, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slSubToolCount,[SubToolGetCount]]
			[If, #slSelSubTool < 0 ,
				[VarSet, slSelSubTool, 0]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 6300, 0,0,0,0]
			[If, #slSelSubTool >= #slSubToolCount ,
				[VarSet, slSelSubTool, (#slSubToolCount -1)]
			,]
			
			[SubToolSelect,#slSelSubTool ]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 6306, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			[If, #slDelCount == 0 ,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay,  "Nothing has been deleted."]
				[LoopExit]
			,
			
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[If, #slDelCount == 1 ,
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, [StrMerge, #slDelCount," subtool has been deleted."]]
				,
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, [StrMerge, #slDelCount," subtools have been deleted."]]
				]
				
				[LoopExit]
			]
			
		]// END OF MAIN LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
			
		,]
	
	]// END OF IFREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NoteBar, ""]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]

[IButton, CleanMeshQ, "",
	[IKeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_CleanZMeshQ]
	]
	
]



[RoutineDef, J_FullReconstruct,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1]
			//..continue
			[Note, "Reconstructing. Please wait..."]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[VarSet, slLastUndo, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[VarSet, slCtr, 0]
			[VarSet, slSubDivCtr, 0]
			[Loop, 1000,
				[IShow, 1011]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Reconstruct Subdiv"]
				[IHide, 1011]
				[VarSet, slCurrentUndo, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
				[If, #slLastUndo ==  #slCurrentUndo,
					[If, #slSubDivCtr == 0 ,
						[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
						[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The mesh can not be reconstructed. Action cancelled."]
						[IUpdate, 1]
						[LoopExit]
					,]
					[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:SDiv", [IGetMax, "Tool:Geometry:SDiv"]]
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Full reconstruction completed."]
					[IUpdate, 1]
					[LoopExit]
				,
					[VarInc, slSubDivCtr]
				]
				
				[VarSet, slLastUndo, #slCurrentUndo]
				[VarInc, slCtr]
				[If, #slCtr == 1000 ,
					[IUpdate, 1]
					[LoopExit]
				,] 
			]/// END OF LOOP
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
			
		]// END OF MAIN LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
			
		,]
	
	]// END OF IFREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NoteBar, ""]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]

[IButton, FullReconstructMesh, "",
	[IKeyPress,'1',
		[RoutineCall, J_FullReconstruct]
	]

]


[RoutineDef, J_PolishFeaturesByPass,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[If, #jInput == 0 ,
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 0, 0, 1, 0, 1]
			//..continue
			[Note, "Polishing. Please wait..."]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 7279,7325]
			[VarSet, slSubDivLvl, [IGet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv]]
			
			[VarSet, slCoverage, [IGet, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage"]]
			[VarSet, slClstr, [IGet, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr"]]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7282, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage", 1]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr", .75]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7285, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Masking:GroupVisible"]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7289, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage", #slCoverage]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr", #slClstr]

			[IMOdSet, "Tool:Deformation:Polish By Features", 1]
			[If, #jInput > 0 ,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7297, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntA, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntB, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoCntB > #slUndoCntA,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			[ISet, "Tool:Deformation:Polish By Features", #jInput ]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			
			[IPRess, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7303, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[ISet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter, [IGetMax , "Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
			[MergeUndo]
			[VarSet, slDelMTStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
			[If, #slDelMTStat,
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7311, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			
			[MergeUndo]
			
			[VarSet, slUndoCntA, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntB, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoCntB > #slUndoCntA,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7316, 0, 0, 0,0]
			
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:Switch"]
			[IPRess, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7321, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[VarSet, slDelMTStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
			[If, #slDelMTStat,
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntA, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntB, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoCntB > #slUndoCntA,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Inverse]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7323, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, "Tool:Morph Target:Morph", 100]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Inverse]
			[IPRess, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7325, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			
		]// END OF MAIN LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
			
		,]
	
	]// END OF IFREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NoteBar, ""]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]


, jInput]

[RoutineDef, J_PolishCrispEdgesByPass,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[If, #jInput == 0 ,
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 0, 0, 1, 0, 1]
			//..continue
			[Note, "Polishing. Please wait..."]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 7279,7325]
			[VarSet, slSubDivLvl, [IGet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv]]
			
			[VarSet, slCoverage, [IGet, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage"]]
			[VarSet, slClstr, [IGet, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr"]]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7282, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage", 1]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr", .75]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7285, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Masking:GroupVisible"]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7289, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage", #slCoverage]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr", #slClstr]

			[IMOdSet, "Tool:Deformation:Polish Crisp Edges", 1]
			[If, #jInput > 0 ,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7297, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntA, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntB, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoCntB > #slUndoCntA,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			[ISet, "Tool:Deformation:Polish Crisp Edges", #jInput ]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			
			[IPRess, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7303, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[ISet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter, [IGetMax , "Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
			[MergeUndo]
			[VarSet, slDelMTStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
			[If, #slDelMTStat,
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7311, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			
			[MergeUndo]
			
			[VarSet, slUndoCntA, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntB, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoCntB > #slUndoCntA,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7316, 0, 0, 0,0]
			
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:Switch"]
			[IPRess, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7321, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[VarSet, slDelMTStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]]
			[If, #slDelMTStat,
				[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:StoreMT"]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntA, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[VarSet, slUndoCntB, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[If, #slUndoCntB > #slUndoCntA,
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Inverse]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7323, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, "Tool:Morph Target:Morph", 100]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Morph Target:DelMT"]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:Inverse]
			[IPRess, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[ISet, Tool:Geometry:SDiv, #slSubDivLvl]
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7325, 0, 0, 0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
		]// END OF MAIN LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
			
		,]
	
	]// END OF IFREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NoteBar, ""]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

, jInput]
