[RoutineDef, J_FaceMode,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0]
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slBrushName, ""]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetCurrentBrush, slBrushName]
			[VarSet, slIsSame, 0]
			[RoutineCall,J_CompareStringsIfSame, #slBrushName, "ZModeler", slIsSame]
			[If, !#slIsSame ,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Brush is not ZModeler. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			//...continue
			[Note, "Switching to FACE MODE only.Please wait..."]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 7540,7580]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7541, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_CenterCanvasFrontOrtho]
			[VarSet, slSelTool, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[IPress, Tool:Clone]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7547, 0,0,0,0]
			[ToolSelect, ([ToolGetCount]-1)]
			
			[VArSet, slSaveFaceOp, [MVarGet, jMemFaceOperation, 0]]
			
			[If, #slSaveFaceOp < 99 ,
				
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 1]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 1]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 1]
				[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
				[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
				[IKeyPress, 32,
					[Delay,.001]
					
					[VarSet, slFaceOpName, ""]
					[RoutineCall,J_GetFaceOperationName, #slSaveFaceOp,slFaceOpName]
					[IPress, #slFaceOpName]
					
				,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			,]
			[If, #slSaveFaceOp >= 99 ,
				
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 1]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 1]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 1]
				[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
				[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
				[IKeyPress, 32,
					[Delay,.001]
					[VarSet, slFaceOpID, -1]
					[RoutineCall,J_GetFaceOperation, slFaceOpID]
					[MVarSet, jMemFaceOperation, 0, #slFaceOpID]
				,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			,]
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				
			,0,0]
			
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 3]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7553, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
			[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7556, 0,0,0,0]
			
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				[Delay,.001]
				[VArSet, slSaveEdgeOp, -1]
				[RoutineCall,J_GetEdgeOperation, slSaveEdgeOp]
				
				[If, #slSaveEdgeOp == 8 ,
				
				,
					
					[MVarSet, jMemEdgeOperation,0, #slSaveEdgeOp]
				]
				
				[IPress, "PopUp:EDGE ACTIONS:Do Nothing"]
				
			,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7561, 0,0,0,0]
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				
			,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7565, 0,0,0,0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 2]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
			[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7570, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				[Delay,.001]
				[VArSet, slSavePointOp, -1]
				[RoutineCall,J_GetPointOperation, slSavePointOp]
				[If, #slSavePointOp == 3 ,
				,
					[MVarSet, jMemPointOperation, 0, #slSavePointOp]
				]
				[IPress, "PopUp:POINT ACTION:Do Nothing"]
				
			,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7576, 0,0,0,0]
			[IKeyPress, '2',
				[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Delete"]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7580, 0,0,0,0]
			[ToolSelect, #slSelTool]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
		]
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1,
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
		,]
		
		[IShow, 1011]

	]
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NoteBar, ""]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]

[IButton, FaceMode,"",
	[IKeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_FaceMode]
	
	]

]

[RoutineDef, J_EdgeMode,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0]
			//...continue
			
			[VarSet, slBrushName, ""]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetCurrentBrush, slBrushName]
			[VarSet, slIsSame, 0]
			[RoutineCall,J_CompareStringsIfSame, #slBrushName, "ZModeler", slIsSame]
			[If, !#slIsSame ,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Brush is not ZModeler. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			//...continue
			[Note, "Switching to EDGE MODE only.Please wait..."]
			[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, 0]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 7540,7580]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7541, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_CenterCanvasFrontOrtho]
			[VarSet, slSelTool, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[IPress, Tool:Clone]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7547, 0,0,0,0]
			[ToolSelect, ([ToolGetCount]-1)]
			[VArSet, slSaveEdgeOp, [MVarGet, jMemEdgeOperation, 0]]
			[If, #slSaveEdgeOp < 100 ,
				
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 3]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
				[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
				[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
				[IKeyPress, 32,
					[Delay,.001]
					[VarSet, slEdgeOpName, ""]
					[RoutineCall,J_GetEdgeOperationName, #slSaveEdgeOp,slEdgeOpName]
					
					
					[IPress, #slEdgeOpName]
					
				,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			,]
			[If, #slSaveEdgeOp == 100 ,
				
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 3]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
				[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
				[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
				[IKeyPress, 32,
					[Delay,.001]

					[MVarSet, jMemEdgeOperation, 0, #slEdgeOpID]
				,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			,]
			
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				
			,0,0]
			
			
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 1]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 1]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 1]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7553, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
			[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7556, 0,0,0,0]
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				[Delay,.001]
				[VArSet, slSaveFaceOp, -1]
				[RoutineCall,J_GetFaceOperation, slSaveFaceOp]
				[If, #slSaveFaceOp == 5 ,
				
				,
					[MVarSet, jMemFaceOperation,0, #slSaveFaceOp]
				]
				
				[IPress, "PopUp:POLYGON ACTIONS:Do Nothing"]
				
			,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7561, 0,0,0,0]
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				
			,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7565, 0,0,0,0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 2]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
			[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7570, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				[Delay,.001]
				[VArSet, slSavePointOp, -1]
				[RoutineCall,J_GetPointOperation, slSavePointOp]
				[If, #slSavePointOp == 3 ,
				
				,
					[MVarSet, jMemPointOperation,0, #slSavePointOp]
				]
				
				[IPress, "PopUp:POINT ACTION:Do Nothing"]
				
			,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7576, 0,0,0,0]

				[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Delete"]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7580, 0,0,0,0]

			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
		]
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1,
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
		,]
		[IShow, 1011]

	]
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[NoteBar, ""]
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]

[RoutineDef, J_PointMode,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0]
			//...continue
			
			[VarSet, slBrushName, ""]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetCurrentBrush, slBrushName]
			[VarSet, slIsSame, 0]
			[RoutineCall,J_CompareStringsIfSame, #slBrushName, "ZModeler", slIsSame]
			[If, !#slIsSame ,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Brush is not ZModeler. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			//...continue
			[Note, "Switching to POINT MODE only.Please wait..."]
			[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, 0]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 7540,7580]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7541, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_CenterCanvasFrontOrtho]
			[VarSet, slSelTool, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[IPress, Tool:Clone]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7547, 0,0,0,0]
			[ToolSelect, ([ToolGetCount]-1)]
			
			[VArSet, slSavePointOp, [MVarGet, jMemPointOperation, 0]]
			[If, #slSavePointOp < 100 ,
				
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
				[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
				[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
				[IKeyPress, 32,
					[Delay,.001]
					[VarSet, slPointOpName, ""]
					[RoutineCall,J_GetPointOperationName, #slSavePointOp,slPointOpName]
					[IPress, #slPointOpName]
					
				,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			,]
			[If, #slSavePointOp == 100 ,
				
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
				[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
				[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
				[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
				[IKeyPress, 32,
					[Delay,.001]
					[VarSet, slPointOpID, -1]
					[RoutineCall,J_GetPointOperation, slPointOpID]
					[MVarSet, jMemPointOperation, 0, #slPointOpID]
				,([IGEt, Document:Width] *.5), ([IGEt, Document:Height] *.5)]
			,]
			
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				
			,0,0]
			
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:X Res", 3]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 2]
			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Z Res", 2]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7553, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
			[RoutineCall, J_TrueFitToCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7556, 0,0,0,0]
			[IKeyPress, 32,
				[Delay,.001]

				[If, #slSaveEdgeOp == 8 ,

					[MVarSet, jMemEdgeOperation,0, #slSaveEdgeOp]

			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7561, 0,0,0,0]

			,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 7565, 0,0,0,0]

			[ISet, "Tool:Initialize:Y Res", 1]

[RoutineDef, J_RemeshDec,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemStrokeSettings", 11, 0]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareStringVar, "jmemLastBrush", "lala"]
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemToolSubToolID", 2, 1]
]
[RoutineCall, J_RemeshDec]

[RoutineDef, J_SampleHeight,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay , ""]
	[VarSet, slMessage, ""]
	[VArSet, slHasNoValidityError, 0]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_ResetSleep]
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[VArSet, slHasNoValidityError, 1]
			[VarSet, slArrayOn, 0]
			[VarSet, slNanoMeshOn, 0]
			[VarSet, slArrayFlags, [IgetFlags, "Tool:ArrayMesh:a.Array Mesh"]]
			[If, (#slArrayFlags == 9) || (#slArrayFlags == 11),
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, "Array Mesh"]]
				[VarSet, slArrayOn, 1]
			,]
			[If, #slArrayOn ,
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, " and "]]
			,]
			[VarSet, slNanoStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:NanoMesh:m.Nano Mesh On"]]
			[If, #slNanoStat ,
				[VarSet, slNanoFlags, [IgetFlags,"Tool:NanoMesh:m.Nano Mesh On"]]
				[If, (#slNanoFlags == 9) || (#slNanoFlags == 11),
					[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, "Nano Mesh"]]
					[VarSet, slNanoMeshOn, 1]
				,]
			,]
			[If, ((#slArrayOn == 1) && (#slNanoMeshOn == 0)) || ((#slArrayOn == 0) && (#slNanoMeshOn == 1))  ,
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, " is on. It would may slow the response time."]]
			,]
			[If, (#slArrayOn == 1) && (#slNanoMeshOn == 1)   ,
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, " are on. These would may slow the response time."]]
			,]
		]
	]
	[VarSet, slMessageStr, [StrLength,#slMessage ]]
	[If, #slMessageStr > 0 ,
		[Note, #slMessage]
	,]
	
	[If, #slHasNoValidityError ,
		[IFreeze,
			[Loop, 1,
				[VarSet, slSubToolID, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[VarSet, slToolID, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 0, #slToolID]
				[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 1, #slSubToolID]
				[VarSet, slCurrentBrush, ""]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetCurrentBrush,slCurrentBrush ]
				[RoutineCall, J_WriteStringVar, "jmemLastBrush", #slCurrentBrush]
				[IPress, Brush:Standard]
				[VarSet, slStrokeFlags, [IGetFlags, "Stroke:Curve Mode"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 0, #slStrokeFlags]
				[If, (#slStrokeFlags == 8) || (#slStrokeFlags == 10) || (#slStrokeFlags == 12) || (#slStrokeFlags == 0) ,
					[IPress, "Stroke:Curve Mode"]
				,]
				[VarSet, slAsLineFlags, [IGetFlags, Stroke:AsLine]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 1, #slAsLineFlags]
				[If, (#slAsLineFlags == 9) || (#slAsLineFlags == 11) || (#slAsLineFlags == 13) || (#slAsLineFlags == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:AsLine]
				,]
				[VarSet, slCurveStep, [IGet, Stroke:CurveStep]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 2, #slCurveStep]
				[ISet, Stroke:CurveStep, 0]
				[VarSet, slBend, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Bend]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 3, #slBend]
				[If, (#slBend == 8) || (#slBend == 10) || (#slBend == 12) || (#slBend == 0) ,
					[IPress, Stroke:Bend]
				,]
				[VarSet, slSnap, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Snap]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 4, #slSnap]
				[If, (#slSnap == 9) || (#slSnap == 11) || (#slSnap == 13) || (#slSnap == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:Snap]
				,]
				[VarSet, slElastic, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Elastic]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 5, #slElastic]
				[If, (#slElastic == 9) || (#slElastic == 11) || (#slElastic == 13) || (#slElastic == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:Elastic]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLiquid, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Liquid]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 6, #slLiquid]
				[If, (#slLiquid == 9) || (#slLiquid == 11) || (#slLiquid == 13) || (#slLiquid == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:Liquid]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLockStart, [IGetFlags, "Stroke:Lock Start"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 7, #slLockStart]
				[If, (#slLockStart == 9) || (#slLockStart == 11) || (#slLockStart == 13) || (#slLockStart == 1) ,
					[IUnPress,  "Stroke:Lock Start"]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLockEnd, [IGetFlags, "Stroke:Lock End"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 8, #slLockEnd]
				[If, (#slLockEnd == 9) || (#slLockEnd == 11) || (#slLockEnd == 13) || (#slLockEnd == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, "Stroke:Lock End"]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLazyBrush, [IgetFlags, Stroke:LazyMouse]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 9, #slLazyBrush]
				[If, (#slLazyBrush == 9) || (#slLazyBrush == 11) || (#slLazyBrush == 13) || (#slLazyBrush == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:LazyMouse]
				,]
				[VarSet, slDrawSize, [IGet, "Draw:Draw Size"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 10, #slDrawSize]
				[ISet, "Draw:Draw Size", 1]
				[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
				
				[Notebar, "Click on the mesh and drag to the desired height sample."]
				[IShowActions, 1]
				[IUpdate, 1,1]
				[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEDOWN, "J_SampleHeightPress", 0, 1, 1]
				[LoopExit]
				
			]//END OF LOOP
			
		]// END OF FREEZE
	,]
	
	
	[If, #slHasError,
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
]


[RoutineDef, J_SampleHeightPress,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop,1,
			[VarSet, slEditMode, [IGetFlags, "Transform:Edit"]]
			[If, (#slEditMode == 8) || (#slEditMode == 10) || (#slEditMode == 12) || (#slEditMode == 0),
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The ZTool is currently out of Edit mode. Action cancelled"]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			[VarSet, slToolID, [MVarGet, jmemToolSubToolID, 0]]
			[VarSet, slCurTool, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[If, #slToolID ==  #slCurTool,
				
			,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The ZTool has been changed. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			]
			
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slSubToolID, [MVarGet, jmemToolSubToolID, 1]]
			[VarSet, slCurSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[If, #slSubToolID ==  #slCurSubTool,
				
			,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The subtool selection has been changed. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			]
			//...continue
			
			
			[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 0, #slToolID]
			[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 1, #slSubToolID]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveMousePosition]
			[IShowActions, 1]
			[IUpdate, 1,1]
			[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEUP, "J_SampleHeightUnPress", 0, 1, 0]
			[LoopExit]
		]
	
	]
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[RoutineCall, J_RestoreStrokeEtc]
		[Note, slErrorToDisplay]
		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEvent, #TIMER, ""]
		[IShowActions, 1]
		[IUpdate, 1, 1]
		[LoopExit]
	,]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]


[RoutineDef, J_RestoreStrokeEtc,
	[VarSet, slStrokeFlags, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 0]]
	[If, (#slStrokeFlags == 8) || (#slStrokeFlags == 10) || (#slStrokeFlags == 12)|| (#slStrokeFlags == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:Curve Mode"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:Curve Mode"]
	]
	[VarSet, slAsLineFlags, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 1]]
	[If, (#slAsLineFlags == 8) || (#slAsLineFlags == 10) || (#slAsLineFlags == 12)|| (#slAsLineFlags == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:AsLine"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:AsLine"]
	]
	[VarSet, slCurveStep, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 2]]
	[ISet, "Stroke:Curve Step", #slCurveStep]
	
	[VarSet, slBend, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 3]]
	[If, (#slBend == 8) || (#slBend == 10) || (#slBend == 12)|| (#slBend == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:Bend"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:Bend"]
	]
	[VarSet, slSnap, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 4]]
	[If, (#slSnap == 8) || (#slSnap == 10) || (#slSnap == 12)|| (#slSnap == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:Snap"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:Snap"]
	]
	[VarSet, slElastic, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 5]]
	[If, (#slElastic == 8) || (#slElastic == 10) || (#slElastic == 12)|| (#slElastic == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:Elastic"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:Elastic"]
	]
	[VarSet, slLiquid, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 6]]
	[If, (#slLiquid == 8) || (#slLiquid == 10) || (#slLiquid == 12)|| (#slLiquid == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:Liquid"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:Liquid"]
	]
	[VarSet, slLockStart, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 7]]
	[If, (#slLockStart == 8) || (#slLockStart == 10) || (#slLockStart == 12)|| (#slLockStart == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:Lock Start"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:Lock Start"]
	]
	[VarSet, slLockEnd, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 8]]
	[If, (#slLockEnd == 8) || (#slLockEnd == 10) || (#slLockEnd == 12)|| (#slLockEnd == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:Lock End"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:Lock End"]
	]
	[VarSet, slLazyMouse, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 9]]
	[If, (#slLazyMouse == 8) || (#slLazyMouse == 10) || (#slLazyMouse == 12)|| (#slLazyMouse == 0)  ,
		[IUnPress, "Stroke:LazyMouse"]
	,
		[IPress, "Stroke:LazyMouse"]
	]
	
	[VarSet, slDrawSize, [MVarGet, jmemStrokeSettings, 10]]
	[IKeyPress, ,
		[ISet, "Draw:Draw Size", #slDrawSize]
	, -500, -500]
	
	
	[VarSet, slLastBrush, ""]
	[RoutineCall, J_WriteStringVar,"jmemLastBrush", slLastBrush]
	[IPress, [StrMerge, "Brush:", #slLastBrush]]
	[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 0, -1]
	[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 1, -1]
	[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
]


[RoutineDef, J_SampleHeightUnPress,
	[IShowActions , 0]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[VarSet, slSavedXPos, 0]
			[VarSet, slSavedYPos, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetSavedMousePosition, slSavedXPos,slSavedYPos]
			[VArSet, slCurMouseX, [MouseHPos]]
			[VArSet, slCurMouseY, [MouseVPos]]
			[VArSet, slTX, 0]
			[VArSet, slTY, 0]
			[VArSet, slTZ, 0]
			[VArSet, slSX, 0]
			[VArSet, slSY, 0]
			[VArSet, slSZ, 0]
			[VArSet, slRX, 0]
			[VArSet, slRY, 0]
			[VArSet, slRZ, 0]
			[TransformGet, slTX, slTY, slTZ, slSX, slSY, slSZ, slRX, slRY, slRZ ]
			[VarSet, slPoint1, ]
			[RoutineCall, J_NewPoint2D, slPoint1,#slSavedXPos, #slSavedYPos ]
			[VarSet, slPoint2, ]
			[RoutineCall, J_NewPoint2D, slPoint2,#slCurMouseX, #slCurMouseY ]
			[VArSet, slVect1,]
			[RoutineCall, J_NewVector2D, slVect1,slPoint1, slPoint2 ]
			[VarSet, slDist, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetVector2DLength,slVect1, slDist]
			[VarSet, slFact, ((100/#slSX)*(#slDist/100))]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Thickness", #slFact]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreStrokeEtc]
			
			[IShowActions, 1]
			[IUpdate, 1,1]
			[Note, [StrMerge, "Panel Loops Thickness is set to ", #slFact]]
			[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEvent, #TIMER, ""]
			[LoopExit]
		]
	
	]

]

	[IKeyPress, '1',

[ISwitch, FlatEdges,0,"",, ,0, 80]
[ISwitch, PanelGrpAppend,0,"",, ,0, 80]
[ISlider, PanelGrpBevel , 40, 1, 0, 100,,, 0, 200 ]
[RoutineDef, J_PanelGrpAdv,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchGeometryError,slHasError, slErrorToDisplay, 0,1,1,0, 1 ]
			//...continue
			
			[Note, "Extracting the panel group/s. Please wait..."]
			
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			
			[VarSet, slToSeparate, 1]
			[VarSet, slPanelGrpAppend, [IGetFlags, "ZScript:PanelGrpAppend"]]
			[If, (#slPanelGrpAppend == 9) || (#slPanelGrpAppend == 11)|| (#slPanelGrpAppend == 13) || (#slPanelGrpAppend == 1)  ,
				[VarSet, slToSeparate, 0]
				[VarSet, slSubDivMax, [IGetMax, "Tool:Geometry:SDiv"]]
				[If, #slSubDivMax > 1 ,
					[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
					[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "Action cancelled.Panels cannot be appended on meshes with subdivision levels. Toggle off the PanelGrpAppend option to make a separate mesh and try again."]
					[LoopExit]
				,]
			,
				
			]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_InitiateProgressBar, 8534, 8623]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8535, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slDelLowerStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Lower"]]
			[If, #slDelLowerStat ,
				[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Del Lower" ]
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8541, 0,0,0,0]
			[VarSet, slDelHigherStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:Geometry:Del Higher"]]
			
			[If, #slDelHigherStat ,
				
				[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Del Higher" ]
				[MergeUndo]
			,]
			
			[VArSet, slFlatEdges, [IGetFlags, "ZScript:FlatEdges"]]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPoints, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPoints, 0]
			[IPress, "Tool:Visibility:HidePt"]
			[VarSet, slHiddenPointsB, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8552, 0,0,0,0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsInfo, slHiddenPointsB, 0]
			
			[If, #slHiddenPoints ==  #slHiddenPointsB,
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "There is no mask. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			
			[If, (#slFlatEdges == 9) || (#slFlatEdges == 11) || (#slFlatEdges == 13) || (#slFlatEdges == 1),
				[MergeUndo]
				[ISet,"Tool:Deformation:Inflate",.4]
			,
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress,"Tool:Visibility:Grow"]
			]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8562, 0,0,0,0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Del Hidden"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Clear"]

			[VarSet, slPolyCov, [IGet, Tool:Polygroups:Coverage]]
			[VarSet, slPolyClstr, [IGet, Tool:Polygroups:Clstr]]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Coverage, 1]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Clstr, .75]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Polygroups:GroupVisible]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Coverage, #slPolyCov]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Clstr, .#slPolyClstr]
			
			[VarSet, slLoopCount, [Iget, "Tool:Geometry:Loops"]]
			[VArSet, slGPolish, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:GPolish"]]
			[VarSet, slGPolishMod, [IModGet, "Tool:Geometry:GPolish"]]
			[VArSet, slTrianglesFlag, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Triangle"]]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Loops", 1]
			[IModSet, "Tool:Geometry:GPolish", 1]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:GPolish", 60]
			[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Triangle"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:GroupsLoops"]
			[ISet,  "Tool:Geometry:Loops", #slLoopCount]
			[ISet,  "Tool:Geometry:GPolish", #slGPolish]
			[IModSet, "Tool:Geometry:GPolish", #slGPolishMod]
			[If, (#slTrianglesFlag == 8) || (#slTrianglesFlag == 12) || (#slTrianglesFlag == 10),
				[IUnPress,"Tool:Geometry:Triangle"]
			,]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Coverage, 1]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Clstr, .75]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Polygroups:GroupVisible]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Coverage, #slPolyCov]
			[ISet, Tool:Polygroups:Clstr, .#slPolyClstr]
			
			
			
			[IModSet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Polish", 1]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Polish", 5]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Loops", 5]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8577, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.Double"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.Ignore Groups"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.RegroupPanels"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.RegroupLoops"]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Elevation", 100]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8583, 0,0,0,0]
			[If, (#slFlatEdges == 9) || (#slFlatEdges == 11) || (#slFlatEdges == 13) || (#slFlatEdges == 1),
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Bevel", 0]
			,
				[VarSet, slScriptBevel, [IGet, "ZScript:PanelGrpBevel"]]
				[VarSet, slThickness, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Thickness"]]
				[VarSet, slFac,(((5 - #slThickness)/5)*#slScriptBevel)]
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Bevel", #slFac]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8593, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:P.Append]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.Panel Loops"]
			[VarSet,slPolishMod, [IModGet, "Tool:Deformation:Polish By Features"]]
			[IModSet, "Tool:Deformation:Polish By Features", 1]
			[MergeUndo]
			[Iset, "Tool:Deformation:Polish By Features",20]
			[IModSet, "Tool:Deformation:Polish By Features", #slPolishMod]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8597, 0,0,0,0]
			[If, #slToSeparate ,
				[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Duplicate]
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:DelUH]
				[SubToolSelect, ([SubToolGetActiveIndex] -1)]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]
				[IPress,Edit:Tool:Undo]
			,
				
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Append"]
				[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressUpdate, 8623, 0,0,0,0]
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_ProgressEnd]
		]
		
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow, 1011]
		,]
	]
	
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
]

[RoutineDef, J_SampleBandWidth,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay , ""]
	[VarSet, slMessage, ""]
	[VArSet, slHasNoValidityError, 0]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_ResetSleep]
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[VArSet, slHasNoValidityError, 1]
			[VarSet, slArrayOn, 0]
			[VarSet, slNanoMeshOn, 0]
			[VarSet, slArrayFlags, [IgetFlags, "Tool:ArrayMesh:a.Array Mesh"]]
			[If, (#slArrayFlags == 9) || (#slArrayFlags == 11),
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, "Array Mesh"]]
				[VarSet, slArrayOn, 1]
			,]
			[If, #slArrayOn ,
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, " and "]]
			,]
			[VarSet, slNanoStat, [IGetStatus, "Tool:NanoMesh:m.Nano Mesh On"]]
			[If, #slNanoStat ,
				[VarSet, slNanoFlags, [IgetFlags,"Tool:NanoMesh:m.Nano Mesh On"]]
				[If, (#slNanoFlags == 9) || (#slNanoFlags == 11),
					[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, "Nano Mesh"]]
					[VarSet, slNanoMeshOn, 1]
				,]
			,]
			[If, ((#slArrayOn == 1) && (#slNanoMeshOn == 0)) || ((#slArrayOn == 0) && (#slNanoMeshOn == 1))  ,
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, " is on. It would may slow the response time."]]
			,]
			[If, (#slArrayOn == 1) && (#slNanoMeshOn == 1)   ,
				[VarSet, slMessage, [StrMerge, #slMessage, " are on. These would may slow the response time."]]
			,]
		]
	]
	[VarSet, slMessageStr, [StrLength,#slMessage ]]
	[If, #slMessageStr > 0 ,
		[Note, #slMessage]
	,]
	
	[If, #slHasNoValidityError ,
		[IFreeze,
			[Loop, 1,
				[VarSet, slSubToolID, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[VarSet, slToolID, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
				[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 0, #slToolID]
				[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 1, #slSubToolID]
				[VarSet, slCurrentBrush, ""]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetCurrentBrush,slCurrentBrush ]
				[RoutineCall, J_WriteStringVar, "jmemLastBrush", #slCurrentBrush]
				[IPress, Brush:Standard]
				[VarSet, slStrokeFlags, [IGetFlags, "Stroke:Curve Mode"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 0, #slStrokeFlags]
				[If, (#slStrokeFlags == 8) || (#slStrokeFlags == 10) || (#slStrokeFlags == 12) || (#slStrokeFlags == 0) ,
					[IPress, "Stroke:Curve Mode"]
				,]
				[VarSet, slAsLineFlags, [IGetFlags, Stroke:AsLine]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 1, #slAsLineFlags]
				[If, (#slAsLineFlags == 9) || (#slAsLineFlags == 11) || (#slAsLineFlags == 13) || (#slAsLineFlags == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:AsLine]
				,]
				[VarSet, slCurveStep, [IGet, Stroke:CurveStep]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 2, #slCurveStep]
				[ISet, Stroke:CurveStep, 0]
				[VarSet, slBend, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Bend]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 3, #slBend]
				[If, (#slBend == 8) || (#slBend == 10) || (#slBend == 12) || (#slBend == 0) ,
					[IPress, Stroke:Bend]
				,]
				[VarSet, slSnap, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Snap]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 4, #slSnap]
				[If, (#slSnap == 9) || (#slSnap == 11) || (#slSnap == 13) || (#slSnap == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:Snap]
				,]
				[VarSet, slElastic, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Elastic]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 5, #slElastic]
				[If, (#slElastic == 9) || (#slElastic == 11) || (#slElastic == 13) || (#slElastic == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:Elastic]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLiquid, [IGetFlags, Stroke:Liquid]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 6, #slLiquid]
				[If, (#slLiquid == 9) || (#slLiquid == 11) || (#slLiquid == 13) || (#slLiquid == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:Liquid]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLockStart, [IGetFlags, "Stroke:Lock Start"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 7, #slLockStart]
				[If, (#slLockStart == 9) || (#slLockStart == 11) || (#slLockStart == 13) || (#slLockStart == 1) ,
					[IUnPress,  "Stroke:Lock Start"]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLockEnd, [IGetFlags, "Stroke:Lock End"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 8, #slLockEnd]
				[If, (#slLockEnd == 9) || (#slLockEnd == 11) || (#slLockEnd == 13) || (#slLockEnd == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, "Stroke:Lock End"]
				,]
				[VarSet, slLazyBrush, [IgetFlags, Stroke:LazyMouse]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 9, #slLazyBrush]
				[If, (#slLazyBrush == 9) || (#slLazyBrush == 11) || (#slLazyBrush == 13) || (#slLazyBrush == 1) ,
					[IUnPress, Stroke:LazyMouse]
				,]
				[VarSet, slDrawSize, [IGet, "Draw:Draw Size"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemStrokeSettings, 10, #slDrawSize]
				[ISet, "Draw:Draw Size", 1]
				[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
				
				[Notebar, "Draw the desired band width sample on the mesh."]
				[IShowActions, 1]
				[IUpdate, 1,1]
				[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEDOWN, "J_SampleBandWidthPress", 0, 1, 1]
				[LoopExit]
				
			]//END OF LOOP
			
		]// END OF FREEZE
	,]
	
	
	[If, #slHasError,
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
	,]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
]


[RoutineDef, J_SampleBandWidthPress,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop,1,
			[VarSet, slEditMode, [IGetFlags, "Transform:Edit"]]
			[If, (#slEditMode == 8) || (#slEditMode == 10) || (#slEditMode == 12) || (#slEditMode == 0),
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The ZTool is currently out of Edit mode. Action cancelled"]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			[VarSet, slToolID, [MVarGet, jmemToolSubToolID, 0]]
			[VarSet, slCurTool, [ToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[If, #slToolID ==  #slCurTool,
				
			,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The ZTool has been changed. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			]
			
			//...continue
			[VarSet, slSubToolID, [MVarGet, jmemToolSubToolID, 1]]
			[VarSet, slCurSubTool, [SubToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[If, #slSubToolID ==  #slCurSubTool,
				
			,
				[VarSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, "The subtool selection has been changed. Action cancelled."]
				[LoopExit]
			]
			//...continue
			
			
			[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 0, #slToolID]
			[MVarSet, jmemToolSubToolID, 1, #slSubToolID]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveMousePosition]
			[IShowActions, 1]
			[IUpdate, 1,1]
			[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEUP, "J_SampleBandWidthUnPress", 0, 1, 0]
			[LoopExit]
		]
	
	]
	[If, #slHasError ,
		[RoutineCall, J_RestoreStrokeEtc]
		[Note, slErrorToDisplay]
		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEvent, #TIMER, ""]
		[IShowActions, 1]
		[IUpdate, 1, 1]
		[LoopExit]
	,]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1, 1]

]



[RoutineDef, J_SampleBandWidthUnPress,
	[IShowActions , 0]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[VarSet, slSavedXPos, 0]
			[VarSet, slSavedYPos, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetSavedMousePosition, slSavedXPos,slSavedYPos]
			[VArSet, slCurMouseX, [MouseHPos]]
			[VArSet, slCurMouseY, [MouseVPos]]
			[VArSet, slTX, 0]
			[VArSet, slTY, 0]
			[VArSet, slTZ, 0]
			[VArSet, slSX, 0]
			[VArSet, slSY, 0]
			[VArSet, slSZ, 0]
			[VArSet, slRX, 0]
			[VArSet, slRY, 0]
			[VArSet, slRZ, 0]
			[TransformGet, slTX, slTY, slTZ, slSX, slSY, slSZ, slRX, slRY, slRZ ]
			[VarSet, slPoint1, ]
			[RoutineCall, J_NewPoint2D, slPoint1,#slSavedXPos, #slSavedYPos ]
			[VarSet, slPoint2, ]
			[RoutineCall, J_NewPoint2D, slPoint2,#slCurMouseX, #slCurMouseY ]
			[VArSet, slVect1,]
			[RoutineCall, J_NewVector2D, slVect1,slPoint1, slPoint2 ]
			[VarSet, slDist, 0]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetVector2DLength,slVect1, slDist]
			[VarSet, slMult, 1]
			[If, #slSX > 100 ,
				[VarSet, slMult, (100/#slSX )]
			,]
			[If, #slSX < 100 ,
				[VarSet, slMult, (#slSX / 100)]
				
			,]
			[VarSet, slFact, INT((INT(#slDist))* #slMult)]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreStrokeEtc]
			
			[IPress, Brush:Standard]
			[ISet, "Draw:Focal Shift", 0]
			[VArSet, slDrawSize, INT(#slFact * 5.2)]
			[ISet, "Draw:Draw Size", #slDrawSize]
			
			[IShowActions, 1]
			[IUpdate, 1,1]
			[NoteBar, [StrMerge, "Brush size is set to ", #slDrawSize]]
			[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEvent, #TIMER, ""]
			[LoopExit]
		]
	
	]

]
[IButton, SampleBandWidth, "",
	[IKeyPress, '1',
		[RoutineCall, J_SampleBandWidth]
	]
]


[RoutineDef, J_SHIFTCTRLBrushDec,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemSHIFTCTRLBrushSettings", 3, 1]
]
[RoutineCall, J_SHIFTCTRLBrushDec]

[RoutineDef, J_CreateBand,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[RoutineCall, J_CatchValidityError, slHasError, slErrorToDisplay]
			//..continue
			[Note, "Please wait..."]
			[IHide, 1011]
			[RoutineCall, J_ResetSleep]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SaveUndoPoint]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit] // Exit edit mode
			[RoutineCall, J_BakeGeometry]
			[VarSet, slDecimateSet, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Polygons Size"]]
			[VarSet, slTesselateFlags,[IGetFlags,"Tool:Geometry:Tesselate"]]
			[VarSet, slDecimateFlags,[IGetFlags,"Tool:Geometry:Decimate"]]
			[If, (#slTesselateFlags == 9) || (#slTesselateFlags == 11) || (#slTesselateFlags == 13) || (#slTesselateFlags == 1),
				[IUnPress,"Tool:Geometry:Tesselate"]
			,]
			[If, (#slDecimateFlags == 8) || (#slDecimateFlags == 10) || (#slDecimateFlags == 12) || (#slDecimateFlags == 0),
				[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Decimate"]
			,]
			[VarSet, slTessimateMod, [IModGet, "Tool:Geometry:Tessimate"]]
			[IModSet, "Tool:Geometry:Tessimate", 0]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Polygons Size", .4]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Tessimate"]
						
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Decimate"]
			[IModSet, "Tool:Geometry:Tessimate", #slTessimateMod]
			[If, (#slTesselateFlags == 9) || (#slTesselateFlags == 11) || (#slTesselateFlags == 13) || (#slTesselateFlags == 1),
				[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Tesselate"]
			,]
			[If, (#slDecimateFlags == 9) || (#slDecimateFlags == 11) || (#slDecimateFlags == 13) || (#slDecimateFlags == 1),
				[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Decimate"]
			,]
			
			//Checks if the symmetry is on then turns it off if yes.
			[VarSet, slSymmetryFlag, [IGetFlags, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"]]
			[If, (#slSymmetryFlag == 9) || (#slSymmetryFlag == 11) || (#slSymmetryFlag == 13) || (#slSymmetryFlag == 1),
				[IUnPress, "Transform:Activate Symmetry"] // Turns off the symmetry.
			,]
			
			[IPress, Transform:Edit]
			[VarSet, slBRadiusFlags, [IGetFlags, "Brush:Clip Brush Modifiers:BRadius"]]
			[If, (#slBRadiusFlags == 8) || (#slBRadiusFlags == 10) || (#slBRadiusFlags == 12) || (#slBRadiusFlags == 0),
				[IPRess, "Brush:Clip Brush Modifiers:BRadius"] // Unpress the BRadius switch if it is currently on. Restore the setting after the action.
			,]
			
			[MVarSet, jmemSHIFTCTRLBrushSettings, 0, #slBRadiusFlags]
			//Query the SHIFT + CTRL brush then
			[VarSet, slShiftCtrlBrush, ""]
			[VarSet, slShiftCtrlCenter, 0]
			[VarSet, slShiftCtrlSquare, 0]
			[VarSet, slBRadiusFlags, 0]
			[VarSet, slFocalShift, 0]
			[VarSet, slBrushID, 0]
			[IKeyPress, SHIFT+CTRL,
				[IClick, "Brush:Current Brush", 0,0]
				[VarSet, slBrushID, [IGet, "Brush:Item Info"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemSHIFTCTRLBrushSettings, 2, #slBrushID]
			]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetSHIFTCTRLBrush, slShiftCtrlBrush, slShiftCtrlCenter, slShiftCtrlSquare]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSHIFTCTRLBrush, "SliceCurve", 0, 0]
			
			[IKeyPress, SHIFT + CTRL,
				[VarSet, slFocalShift, [IGet, "Draw:Focal Shift"]]
				[MVarSet, jmemSHIFTCTRLBrushSettings, 1, #slFocalShift]
				[ISet, "Draw:Focal Shift", -100]
			]
			
			// Sleep section
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			
			
		]// END LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloRestore]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[IUnPress, Draw:Perspective]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow,1011]
			
		,]
	]//END FREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError == 1,
		[Note, slErrorToDisplay]
	,
	
	]
	
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
	[If, #slHasError == 0 ,
		[NoteBar, "Use the SliceCurve to create a band mesh. Hold SHIFT + CTRL to draw the curve."]
		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEDOWN,"J_CreateBandPressed", 3, 1, 0]
	,
		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEvent, #TIMER,""]
	
	]
]
[RoutineDef, J_CreateBandDec,
	[RoutineCall, J_DeclareVarQuiet, "jmemCreateBandCurUndo", 1, 1]
]

[RoutineCall, J_CreateBandDec]
[RoutineDef, J_CreateBandPressed,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[Loop, 1,
		[VArSet, slCtrlShiftPressed, 0]
		[RoutineCall, J_CtrlShiftGetStatus, slCtrlShiftPressed]
		[IShowActions, 1]
		[If, #slCtrlShiftPressed,
			[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEUP,"J_CreateBandUnPressed", 0, 0, 0]
		,
			[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEDOWN,"J_CreateBandPressed", 3, 1, 0]

		]
	
	]	

]


[RoutineDef, J_CreateBandUnPressed,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VarSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[VarSet, slAutoCollapseFlags, -1]
	[VArSet, slISSuccess, 0]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[Note, "Please wait..."]
			[IHide, 1011]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_SetUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_SetSaveScrollBarStatus]
			[RoutineCall, J_GetAutoCollapseFlags, slAutoCollapseFlags]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_StoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOn]
			[VarSet, slLastStroke,[StrokeGetLast] ]
			[VarSet, slBrushSize, [IGet, "Draw:Draw Size"]]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			
			[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
			[VarSet, slToolLast,[ToolGetActiveIndex]]
			[IPress, Tool:Clone]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[IPress,Tool:Masking:Clear]
			[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
			[ToolSelect,([ToolGetCount] - 1)]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Coverage", 1]
			[ISet, "Tool:Polygroups:Clstr", .75]
			[IPress, "Tool:Polygroups:GroupVisible"]
			
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[MergeUndo]
			[CanvasStroke, #slLastStroke]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[VarSet,slHasGroups, 0]
			[VArSet, slUndoStepBefore, [IGet,"Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
			[VarSet,slBorderMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			[VarSet,slGroupsMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			[VarSet,slCreaseMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Border"]
			[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:MaskByFeature]
			[VArSet, slUndoStepAfter, [IGet,"Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
			[If, (#slBorderMask == 9) || (#slBorderMask == 11) || (#slBorderMask == 13) || (#slBorderMask == 1),
				[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Border"]
			,]
			[If, (#slGroupsMask == 8) || (#slGroupsMask == 10) || (#slGroupsMask == 12) || (#slGroupsMask == 0),
				[IUnPress,"Tool:Masking:Groups"]
			,]
			[If, (#slCreaseMask == 9) || (#slCreaseMask == 11) || (#slCreaseMask == 13) || (#slCreaseMask == 1),
				[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Crease"]
			,]
			[If, #slUndoStepAfter > #slUndoStepBefore ,
				[VArSet, slHasGroups, 1]
			,]
			
			[If, #slHasGroups ,
				[MergeUndo]
				[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Unweld Groups Border]
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:DelUH]
				[IKeyPress, '2',
					[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Groups Split"]
				]
			,
			
				[IKeyPress,'2',
					[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Del All"]
				]
				[VArSet, slHasError, 1]
				
				[VArSet, slErrorToDisplay, " Nothing has been sliced. Try Again."]
				[ToolSelect, #slToolLast]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[IPress,Tool:Masking:Clear]
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
				[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
				[LoopExit]
			]
			
			
			
			[VarSet, slSubToolCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
			[If, #slSubToolCount == 2 ,
				[IKeyPress,'2',
					[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Del All"]
				]
				[ToolSelect, #slToolLast]
				[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
				[IPress,Tool:Masking:Clear]
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
				[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
				[VArSet, slHasError, 1]
				[VArSet, slErrorToDisplay, " The slice is not properly done. Try again."]
				[LoopExit]
			,]
			
			//...continue
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[VarSet, slCtr,0]
			[VarSet, slDrawSize,[IGet,"Draw:Draw Size"]]
			[ISet,"Draw:Draw Size", (#slDrawSize * .8)]
			[VArSet, slTheMid,0]
			[Loop, #slSubToolCount,
				[SubToolSelect, #slCtr]
				[VarSet,slPCount, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsCount, slPCount]
				[CanvasStroke, #slLastStroke]
				[VarSet,slPCount2, 0]
				[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsCount, slPCount2]
				[If, #slPCount2 ==  #slPCount,
					
				,
					[VArSet, slTheMid,#slCtr]
					[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
					[IPress,Edit:Tool:DelUH]
					[LoopExit]
				]
				[VarInc, slCtr]
			]
			[ISet,"Draw:Draw Size", #slDrawSize]
			
			[SubToolSelect,#slTheMid]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[IKeyPress, '2',
				[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Del Other"]
			]  
			[MergeUndo]
			[Ipress, "Tool:Initialize:QCube"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Polygroups:Auto Groups"]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Copy]
			[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Paste Append"]
			[VArSet, slHasOtherGroups, 0]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Clear"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, Tool:Geometry:Unweld Groups Border]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Polygroups:Auto Groups"]
			
			[VArSet, slUndoStepBefore, [IGet,"Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
			[VarSet,slBorderMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
			[VarSet,slGroupsMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
			[VarSet,slCreaseMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
			[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Border"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]
			[IPress, Tool:Masking:MaskByFeature]
			[VArSet, slUndoStepAfter, [IGet,"Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
			[If, (#slBorderMask == 8) || (#slBorderMask == 10) || (#slBorderMask == 12) || (#slBorderMask == 0),
				[IUnPress,"Tool:Masking:Border"]
			,]
			[If, (#slGroupsMask == 9) || (#slGroupsMask == 11) || (#slGroupsMask == 13) || (#slGroupsMask == 1),
				[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Groups"]
			,]
			[If, (#slCreaseMask == 9) || (#slCreaseMask == 11) || (#slCreaseMask == 13) || (#slCreaseMask == 1),
				[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Crease"]
			,]
			[If, #slUndoStepAfter > #slUndoStepBefore ,
				[VArSet, slHasOtherGroups, 1]
			,]
		
			[If, #slHasOtherGroups ,
				[IKeyPress, 32,
					[IKeyPress, '2',
						[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Split To Parts"]
					]
					[VarSet, slSelectedSub,[SubtoolGetActiveIndex]]
					[SubToolSelect, ([SubToolGetCount] - 1)]
					[IKeyPress, '2',
						[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Delete"]
					]
					[SubToolSelect, #slSelectedSub]
				]
				
				[VarSet,#slNewCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
				
				[If, #slNewCount >1 ,
					[VarSet, slDrawSize,[IGet,"Draw:Draw Size"]]
					[ISet,"Draw:Draw Size", (#slDrawSize * .8)]
					[VarSet, slCtr,0]
					[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
					[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
					[VarSet, slFirstCopy,0]
					[Loop, #slNewCount,
						[SubToolSelect, #slCtr]
						[VarSet,slPCount, 0]
						[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsCount, slPCount]
						[CanvasStroke, #slLastStroke]
						[VarSet,slPCount2, 0]
						[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsCount, slPCount2]
						[If, #slPCount2 == #slPCount ,
							
						,
							[If, #slFirstCopy == 0,
								[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
								[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Copy"]
								[VarSet, slFirstCopy,1]
							,
								[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
								[MergeUndo]
								[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Paste Append"]
								[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Copy"]
								[IPress,"Edit:Tool:DelUH"]
							]
						]
						[VarInc, #slCtr]
					]
					[ISet,"Draw:Draw Size", #slDrawSize]
					[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
					[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
					[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
				,]
				
			,
				[VArSet, slUndoStepBefore, [IGet,"Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
				[VarSet,slBorderMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Border"]]
				[VarSet,slGroupsMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]]
				[VarSet,slCreaseMask, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]]
				[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Border"]
				[IPress, "Tool:Masking:Groups"]
				[IUnPress, "Tool:Masking:Crease"]
				[IPress, Tool:Masking:MaskByFeature]
				[VArSet, slUndoStepAfter, [IGet,"Edit:Tool:UndoCounter"]]
				[If, (#slBorderMask == 9) || (#slBorderMask == 11) || (#slBorderMask == 13) || (#slBorderMask == 1),
					[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Border"]
				,]
				[If, (#slGroupsMask == 8) || (#slGroupsMask == 10) || (#slGroupsMask == 12) || (#slGroupsMask == 0),
					[IUnPress,"Tool:Masking:Groups"]
				,]
				[If, (#slCreaseMask == 9) || (#slCreaseMask == 11) || (#slCreaseMask == 13) || (#slCreaseMask == 1),
					[IPress,"Tool:Masking:Crease"]
				,]
				[VarSet, slHasGroups2, 0]
				[If, #slUndoStepAfter > #slUndoStepBefore ,
					[VArSet, slHasGroups2, 1]
				,]
				[If, #slHasGroups2 ,
					[IKeyPress, '2',
						[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Groups Split"]
					]
					[VarSet,#slNewCount, [SubToolGetCount]]
					[If, #slNewCount > 1 ,
						[VarSet, slFirstCopy,0]
						[VarSet, slDrawSize,[IGet,"Draw:Draw Size"]]
						[ISet,"Draw:Draw Size", (#slDrawSize * .8)]
						[VarSet, slCtr,0]
						[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
						[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
						[Loop, #slNewCount,
							[SubToolSelect, #slCtr]
							[VarSet,slPCount, 0]
							[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsCount, slPCount]
							[CanvasStroke,#slLastStroke]
							[VarSet,slPCount2, 0]
							[RoutineCall, J_GetPointsCount, slPCount2]
							[If, #slPCount2 == #slPCount ,
								
							,
								[If, #slFirstCopy == 0,
									[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
									[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Copy"]
									[VarSet, slFirstCopy,1]
								,
									[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
									[MergeUndo]
									[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Paste Append"]
									[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Copy"]
									[IPress,"Edit:Tool:DelUH"]
								]
								
							]
							[VarInc, #slCtr]
						]
						
						[ISet,"Draw:Draw Size", #slDrawSize]
						[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
						[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
						[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
					,]
					
				,]
			
			]
			[IUnPress, Transform:Edit]
			[RoutineCall, J_HideCanvas]
			[VarSet, slFrBorderFlag, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder"]]
			[VarSet, slFrGroupsFlag, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups"]]
			[VarSet, slKpGroupsFlag, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups"]]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:FreezeBorder"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:FreezeGroups"]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:KeepGroups"]
			[VArSet, slSmGrpBorder, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups"]]
			[ISet,"Tool:Geometry:SmoothGroups", .5 ]
			[VarSet, slTargetPolyCount,[ IGet,"Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count"]]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", 2]
			[VarSet, slAdaptFlags, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:Adapt"]]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:Adapt"]
			[VarSet, slSameFlag,[IGetFlags,"Tool:Geometry:Same" ]]
			[VarSet, slDoubleFlag,[IGetFlags,"Tool:Geometry:Double"]]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Same"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Double"]
			[VarSet, slAdaptiveSize, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize"]]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:AdaptiveSize", 50]
			[VarSet, slCurvesStrength, [IGet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength"]]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Curves Strength", 50]
			[VarSet, slUsePpFlags, [IGetFlags, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:Use Polypaint"]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:ZRemesher"]
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:Target Polygons Count", 5]
			
			
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Loops", 5]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.Append"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.Inner"]
			[ISet,"Tool:Geometry:P.Polish", 5]
			[IMOdSet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Polish", 1]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.Ignore Groups"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.RegroupPanels"]
			[IUnPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.RegroupLoops"]
			[VarSet, slFlatEdgesFlags, [IGetFlags, "ZScript:FlatEdges"]]
			[If, (#slFlatEdgesFlags == 11) || (#slFlatEdgesFlags == 13)  || (#slFlatEdgesFlags == 9)|| (#slFlatEdgesFlags == 1),
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Bevel", 0]
			,
				[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Bevel", [IGet, "ZScript:PanelGrpBevel"]]
			]
			
			[ISet, "Tool:Geometry:P.Elevation", 100]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress, "Tool:Geometry:P.Panel Loops"]
			[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Copy"]
			[IKeyPress, '2',
				[IPress, "Tool:SubTool:Del All"]
			]
			[ToolSelect, #slToolLast]
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoPoint]
			[IPress, Tool:SubTool:Duplicate]
			[MergeUndo]
			[IPress,"Tool:Geometry:Paste Replace"]
			[IPress,Edit:Tool:DelUH]
			[RoutineCall, J_MemoryOptimize, 0]
			[SubToolSelect,([SubToolGetActiveIndex] - 1)]
			[VarSet, slRadiusFlags, [MVarGet,jmemSHIFTCTRLBrushSettings, 0]]
			[If, (#slRadiusFlags == 8) || (#slRadiusFlags == 10) || (#slRadiusFlags == 12) || (#slRadiusFlags == 0),
				[IUnPRess, "Brush:Clip Brush Modifiers:BRadius"]
			,
			]
			[ISet, "Draw:Focal Shift", [MVarGet, jmemSHIFTCTRLBrushSettings, 1]]
			[IkeyPress, '1',
				[VarSet, slShiftCtrlBrushID, [MVarGet, jmemSHIFTCTRLBrushSettings, 2]]
				
				[ISet, "Brush:Item Info",#slShiftCtrlBrushID ]
				
			]
			[ISet, "Draw:Draw Size", #slBrushSize]
			[VArSet, slISSuccess, 1]
			
		] // ----- END OF LOOP
		[If, #slAutoCollapseFlags > -1 ,
			
			[RoutineCall, J_CanvasClickInsertMesh]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreUndoHistoryMax]
			[RoutineCall, J_SoloOff]
			 
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreCameraCanvas]
			[RoutineCall, J_RestoreScrollBarStatus]
			[IShow,1011]
		,]
	]// END OF FREEZE
	
	[If, #slHasError,
		[Note, #slErrorToDisplay]
		
	,]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate, 1,1]
	[If, #slISSuccess ,
		
		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEvent, #TIMER,""]
	,
		
		[NoteBar, "Use the SliceCurve to create a band mesh. Hold SHIFT + CTRL to draw the curve."]
		[RoutineCall, J_SleepChangeEventAdv, #LMOUSEDOWN,"J_CreateBandPressed", 3, 1, 0]
	
	]

[IButton, DeleteUndoCount, "",
	[RoutineCall, J_DeleteUndoCount]

[RoutineDef, J_DeleteUndoCount,
	[IShowActions, 0]
	[VarSet, slHasError, 0]
	[VArSet, slErrorToDisplay, ""]
	[IFreeze,
		[Loop, 1,
			[VarSet, slUndoCnt, [IGet, "ZScript:UndoCountToDelete"]]
			[VArSet, slUndoCounter, [IGet, Edit:Tool:UndoCounter]]
			[VarSet, slUndoHistoryMax, [IGet, "Preferences:Undo History:Max Undo History"]]
			[If, #slUndoCounter <=  #slUndoCnt,
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:DelUH]
				[LoopExit]
				//..continue
			,]
			[ISet, "Preferences:Undo History:Max Undo History", (#slUndoCounter - #slUndoCnt)]
			[IKeyPress, '1',
				[IPress, Tool:Masking:Clear]
				[IPress, Edit:Tool:Undo]
			]
			[ISet, "Preferences:Undo History:Max Undo History", #slUndoHistoryMax]
		]
	
	]
	[IShowActions, 1]
	[IUpdate,1,1]

]

	[TransformSet,#slTX, #slTY, #slTZ, (#slSX), (#slSY ),(#slSZ ), (#slRX ), (#slRY *-1), (#slRZ*-1)]
	[TransformGet, slTX, slTY, slTZ, slSX, slSY,slSZ, slRX, slRY, slRZ]
	[TransformSet,([CanvasPanGetH] + ([IWIDTH,1004] / 2)), #slTY, (#slTZ-(-1*([Iwidth,1004]/2)) ), (#slSX), (#slSY ),(#slSZ ), (#slRX ), (#slRY), (#slRZ)]
	[TransformGet, slTX, slTY, slTZ, slSX, slSY,slSZ, slRX, slRY, slRZ]
	[TransformSet,#slTX, #slTY, #slTZ, (#slSX), (#slSY ),(#slSZ ), (#slRX ), (#slRY), (#slRZ)]

	/*ROUTINES*/
	
// ----- Sets the path of the ZFileUtils -------------
[RoutineDef, LoadFileUtils, 
	[VarSet,slIsMac, [ZBrushInfo,6]]	//check Mac or PC
 	// Make sure we have the dll and set its path
	[If,[ZBrushInfo,16]==64,//64 bit
  		[If,slIsMac,
  	 	
  	 		[VarSet,gFileUtilsPath,"Dll/ZFileUtils.lib"]	
  	 						
 	 	,	
  	 	
  	 		[VarSet,gFileUtilsPath,"Dll/ZFileUtils64.dll"]
  	 	
  		]
	,//else 32 bit
 	 	[If,slIsMac,
  	 	
  	 		[VarSet,gFileUtilsPath,"Dll/ZFileUtils.lib"]	
  	 						
 	 	,	
  	 	
  	 		[VarSet,gFileUtilsPath,"Dll/ZFileUtils64.dll"]
  	 	
  		]
	]
	
]


[RoutineDef, J_EndDec,
	[NoteBar, ""]
	[RoutineCall, LoadFileUtils]	
	[RoutineCall, J_SleepStart]
]
[RoutineCall, J_EndDec]
	
	/*End of ZScript*/
